# The Tengo Language

[![GoDoc](https://godoc.org/github.com/d5/tengo/v2?status.svg)](https://godoc.org/github.com/d5/tengo/v2)
![test](https://github.com/d5/tengo/workflows/test/badge.svg)
[![Go Report Card](https://goreportcard.com/badge/github.com/d5/tengo)](https://goreportcard.com/report/github.com/d5/tengo)

**Tengo is a small, dynamic, fast, secure script language for Go.** 

Tengo is **[fast](#benchmark)** and secure because it's compiled/executed as
bytecode on stack-based VM that's written in native Go.

```golang
/* The Tengo Language */
fmt := import("fmt")

each := func(seq, fn) {
    for x in seq { fn(x) }
}

sum := func(init, seq) {
    each(seq, func(x) { init += x })
    return init
}

fmt.println(sum(0, [1, 2, 3]))   // "6"
fmt.println(sum("", [1, 2, 3]))  // "123"
```

> Test this Tengo code in the
> [Tengo Playground](https://tengolang.com/?s=0c8d5d0d88f2795a7093d7f35ae12c3afa17bea3)

## Features

- Simple and highly readable
  [Syntax](https://github.com/d5/tengo/blob/master/docs/tutorial.md)
  - Dynamic typing with type coercion
  - Higher-order functions and closures
  - Immutable values
- [Securely Embeddable](https://github.com/d5/tengo/blob/master/docs/interoperability.md)
  and [Extensible](https://github.com/d5/tengo/blob/master/docs/objects.md)
- Compiler/runtime written in native Go _(no external deps or cgo)_
- Executable as a
  [standalone](https://github.com/d5/tengo/blob/master/docs/tengo-cli.md)
  language / REPL
- Use cases: rules engine, [state machine](https://github.com/d5/go-fsm),
  data pipeline, [transpiler](https://github.com/d5/tengo2lua)

## Benchmark

| | fib(35) | fibt(35) |  Language (Type)  |

package parser

import (
	"strings"
)

const (
	nullRep = "<null>"
)

// Node represents a node in the AST.
type Node interface {
	// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
	Pos() Pos
	// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
	End() Pos
	// String returns a string representation of the node.
	String() string
}

// IdentList represents a list of identifiers.
type IdentList struct {
	LParen  Pos
	VarArgs bool
	List    []*Ident
	RParen  Pos
}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (n *IdentList) Pos() Pos {
	if n.LParen.IsValid() {
		return n.LParen
	}
	if len(n.List) > 0 {
		return n.List[0].Pos()
	}
	return NoPos
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (n *IdentList) End() Pos {
	if n.RParen.IsValid() {
		return n.RParen + 1
	}
	if l := len(n.List); l > 0 {
		return n.List[l-1].End()
	}
	return NoPos
}

// NumFields returns the number of fields.
func (n *IdentList) NumFields() int {
	if n == nil {
		return 0
	}
	return len(n.List)
}

func (n *IdentList) String() string {
	var list []string
	for i, e := range n.List {
		if n.VarArgs && i == len(n.List)-1 {
			list = append(list, "..."+e.String())
		} else {
			list = append(list, e.String())
		}
	}
	return "(" + strings.Join(list, ", ") + ")"
}
package parser

import (
	"strings"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/token"
)

// Expr represents an expression node in the AST.
type Expr interface {
	Node
	exprNode()
}

// ArrayLit represents an array literal.
type ArrayLit struct {
	Elements []Expr
	LBrack   Pos
	RBrack   Pos
}

func (e *ArrayLit) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *ArrayLit) Pos() Pos {
	return e.LBrack
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *ArrayLit) End() Pos {
	return e.RBrack + 1
}

func (e *ArrayLit) String() string {
	var elements []string
	for _, m := range e.Elements {
		elements = append(elements, m.String())
	}
	return "[" + strings.Join(elements, ", ") + "]"
}

// BadExpr represents a bad expression.
type BadExpr struct {
	From Pos
	To   Pos
}

func (e *BadExpr) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *BadExpr) Pos() Pos {
	return e.From
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *BadExpr) End() Pos {
	return e.To
}

func (e *BadExpr) String() string {
	return "<bad expression>"
}

// BinaryExpr represents a binary operator expression.
type BinaryExpr struct {
	LHS      Expr
	RHS      Expr
	Token    token.Token
	TokenPos Pos
}

func (e *BinaryExpr) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *BinaryExpr) Pos() Pos {
	return e.LHS.Pos()
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *BinaryExpr) End() Pos {
	return e.RHS.End()
}

func (e *BinaryExpr) String() string {
	return "(" + e.LHS.String() + " " + e.Token.String() +
		" " + e.RHS.String() + ")"
}

// BoolLit represents a boolean literal.
type BoolLit struct {
	Value    bool
	ValuePos Pos
	Literal  string
}

func (e *BoolLit) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *BoolLit) Pos() Pos {
	return e.ValuePos
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *BoolLit) End() Pos {
	return Pos(int(e.ValuePos) + len(e.Literal))
}

func (e *BoolLit) String() string {
	return e.Literal
}

// CallExpr represents a function call expression.
type CallExpr struct {
	Func     Expr
	LParen   Pos
	Args     []Expr
	Ellipsis Pos
	RParen   Pos
}

func (e *CallExpr) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *CallExpr) Pos() Pos {
	return e.Func.Pos()
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *CallExpr) End() Pos {
	return e.RParen + 1
}

func (e *CallExpr) String() string {
	var args []string
	for _, e := range e.Args {
		args = append(args, e.String())
	}
	if len(args) > 0 && e.Ellipsis.IsValid() {
		args[len(args)-1] = args[len(args)-1] + "..."
	}
	return e.Func.String() + "(" + strings.Join(args, ", ") + ")"
}

// CharLit represents a character literal.
type CharLit struct {
	Value    rune
	ValuePos Pos
	Literal  string
}

func (e *CharLit) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *CharLit) Pos() Pos {
	return e.ValuePos
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *CharLit) End() Pos {
	return Pos(int(e.ValuePos) + len(e.Literal))
}

func (e *CharLit) String() string {
	return e.Literal
}

// CondExpr represents a ternary conditional expression.
type CondExpr struct {
	Cond        Expr
	True        Expr
	False       Expr
	QuestionPos Pos
	ColonPos    Pos
}

func (e *CondExpr) exprNode() {}

	KeyPos   Pos
	ColonPos Pos
	Value    Expr
}

func (e *MapElementLit) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *MapElementLit) Pos() Pos {
	return e.KeyPos
}

func (e *MapElementLit) End() Pos {
	return e.Value.End()
}

func (e *MapElementLit) String() string {
	return e.Key + ": " + e.Value.String()
}

// MapLit represents a map literal.
type MapLit struct {
	LBrace   Pos
	Elements []*MapElementLit
	RBrace   Pos
}

func (e *MapLit) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *MapLit) Pos() Pos {
	return e.LBrace
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *MapLit) End() Pos {
	return e.RBrace + 1
}

func (e *MapLit) String() string {
	var elements []string
	for _, m := range e.Elements {
		elements = append(elements, m.String())
	}
	return "{" + strings.Join(elements, ", ") + "}"
}

// ParenExpr represents a parenthesis wrapped expression.
type ParenExpr struct {
	Expr   Expr
	LParen Pos
	RParen Pos
}

func (e *ParenExpr) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *ParenExpr) Pos() Pos {
	return e.LParen
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *ParenExpr) End() Pos {
	return e.RParen + 1
}

func (e *ParenExpr) String() string {
	return "(" + e.Expr.String() + ")"
}

// SelectorExpr represents a selector expression.
type SelectorExpr struct {
	Expr Expr
	Sel  Expr
}

func (e *SelectorExpr) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *SelectorExpr) Pos() Pos {
	return e.Expr.Pos()
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *SelectorExpr) End() Pos {
	return e.Sel.End()
}

func (e *SelectorExpr) String() string {
	return e.Expr.String() + "." + e.Sel.String()
}

// SliceExpr represents a slice expression.
type SliceExpr struct {
	Expr   Expr
	LBrack Pos
	Low    Expr
	High   Expr
	RBrack Pos
}

func (e *SliceExpr) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *SliceExpr) Pos() Pos {
	return e.Expr.Pos()
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *SliceExpr) End() Pos {
	return e.RBrack + 1
}

func (e *SliceExpr) String() string {
	var low, high string
	if e.Low != nil {
		low = e.Low.String()
	}
	if e.High != nil {
		high = e.High.String()
	}
	return e.Expr.String() + "[" + low + ":" + high + "]"
}

// StringLit represents a string literal.
type StringLit struct {
	Value    string
	ValuePos Pos
	Literal  string
}

func (e *StringLit) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *StringLit) Pos() Pos {
	return e.ValuePos
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *StringLit) End() Pos {
	return Pos(int(e.ValuePos) + len(e.Literal))
}

func (e *StringLit) String() string {
	return e.Literal
}

// UnaryExpr represents an unary operator expression.
type UnaryExpr struct {
	Expr     Expr
	Token    token.Token
	TokenPos Pos
}

func (e *UnaryExpr) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *UnaryExpr) Pos() Pos {
	return e.Expr.Pos()
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *UnaryExpr) End() Pos {
	return e.Expr.End()
}

func (e *UnaryExpr) String() string {
	return "(" + e.Token.String() + e.Expr.String() + ")"
}

// UndefinedLit represents an undefined literal.
type UndefinedLit struct {
	TokenPos Pos
}

func (e *UndefinedLit) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *UndefinedLit) Pos() Pos {
	return e.TokenPos
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *UndefinedLit) End() Pos {
	return e.TokenPos + 9 // len(undefined) == 9
}

func (e *UndefinedLit) String() string {
	return "undefined"
}

package token

import "strconv"

var keywords map[string]Token

// Token represents a token.
type Token int

// List of tokens
const (
	Illegal Token = iota
	EOF
	Comment
	_literalBeg
	Ident
	Int
	Float
	Char
	String
	_literalEnd
	_operatorBeg
	Add          // +
	Sub          // -
	Mul          // *
	Quo          // /
	Rem          // %
	And          // &
	Or           // |
	Xor          // ^
	Shl          // <<
	Shr          // >>
	AndNot       // &^
	AddAssign    // +=
	SubAssign    // -=
	MulAssign    // *=
	QuoAssign    // /=
	RemAssign    // %=
	AndAssign    // &=
	OrAssign     // |=
	XorAssign    // ^=
	ShlAssign    // <<=
	ShrAssign    // >>=
	AndNotAssign // &^=
	LAnd         // &&
	LOr          // ||
	Inc          // ++
	Dec          // --
	Equal        // ==
	Less         // <
	Greater      // >
	Assign       // =
	Not          // !
	NotEqual     // !=
	LessEq       // <=
	GreaterEq    // >=
	Define       // :=
	Ellipsis     // ...
	LParen       // (
	LBrack       // [
	LBrace       // {
	Comma        // ,
	Period       // .
	RParen       // )
	RBrack       // ]
	RBrace       // }
	Semicolon    // ;
	Colon        // :
	Question     // ?
	_operatorEnd
	_keywordBeg
	Break
	Continue
	Else
	For
	Func
	Error
	Immutable
	If
	Return
	Export
	True
	False
	In
	Undefined
	Import
	_keywordEnd
)

var tokens = [...]string{
	Illegal:      "ILLEGAL",
	EOF:          "EOF",
	Comment:      "COMMENT",
	Ident:        "IDENT",
	Int:          "INT",
	Float:        "FLOAT",
	Char:         "CHAR",

	Return:       "return",
	Export:       "export",
	True:         "true",
	False:        "false",
	In:           "in",
	Undefined:    "undefined",
	Import:       "import",
}

func (tok Token) String() string {
	s := ""

	if 0 <= tok && tok < Token(len(tokens)) {
		s = tokens[tok]
	}

	if s == "" {
		s = "token(" + strconv.Itoa(int(tok)) + ")"
	}

	return s
}

// LowestPrec represents lowest operator precedence.
const LowestPrec = 0

// Precedence returns the precedence for the operator token.
func (tok Token) Precedence() int {
	switch tok {
	case LOr:
		return 1
	case LAnd:
		return 2
	case Equal, NotEqual, Less, LessEq, Greater, GreaterEq:
		return 3
	case Add, Sub, Or, Xor:
		return 4
	case Mul, Quo, Rem, Shl, Shr, And, AndNot:
		return 5
	}
	return LowestPrec
}

// IsLiteral returns true if the token is a literal.
func (tok Token) IsLiteral() bool {
	return _literalBeg < tok && tok < _literalEnd
}

// IsOperator returns true if the token is an operator.
func (tok Token) IsOperator() bool {
	return _operatorBeg < tok && tok < _operatorEnd
}

// IsKeyword returns true if the token is a keyword.
func (tok Token) IsKeyword() bool {
	return _keywordBeg < tok && tok < _keywordEnd
}

// Lookup returns corresponding keyword if ident is a keyword.
func Lookup(ident string) Token {
	if tok, isKeyword := keywords[ident]; isKeyword {
		return tok
	}
	return Ident
}

func init() {
	keywords = make(map[string]Token)
	for i := _keywordBeg + 1; i < _keywordEnd; i++ {
		keywords[tokens[i]] = i
	}
}

package parser

import (
	"strings"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/token"
)

// Stmt represents a statement in the AST.
type Stmt interface {
	Node
	stmtNode()
}

// AssignStmt represents an assignment statement.
type AssignStmt struct {
	LHS      []Expr
	RHS      []Expr
	Token    token.Token
	TokenPos Pos
}

func (s *AssignStmt) stmtNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (s *AssignStmt) Pos() Pos {
	return s.LHS[0].Pos()
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (s *AssignStmt) End() Pos {
	return s.RHS[len(s.RHS)-1].End()
}

func (s *AssignStmt) String() string {
	var lhs, rhs []string
	for _, e := range s.LHS {
		lhs = append(lhs, e.String())
	}
	for _, e := range s.RHS {
		rhs = append
[OUTPUT_TAIL]

	if s.Label != nil {
		return s.Label.End()
	}

	return Pos(int(s.TokenPos) + len(s.Token.String()))
}

func (s *BranchStmt) String() string {
	var label string
	if s.Label != nil {
		label = " " + s.Label.Name
	}
	return s.Token.String() + label
}

// EmptyStmt represents an empty statement.
type EmptyStmt struct {
	Semicolon Pos
	Implicit  bool
}

func (s *EmptyStmt) stmtNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (s *EmptyStmt) Pos() Pos {
	return s.Semicolon
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (s *EmptyStmt) End() Pos {
	if s.Implicit {
		return s.Semicolon
	}
	return s.Semicolon + 1
}

func (s *EmptyStmt) String() string {
	return ";"
}

// ExportStmt represents an export statement.
type ExportStmt struct {
	ExportPos Pos
	Result    Expr
}

func (s *ExportStmt) stmtNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (s *ExportStmt) Pos() Pos {
	return s.ExportPos
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (s *ExportStmt) End() Pos {
	return s.Result.End()
}

func (s *ExportStmt) String() string {
	return "export " + s.Result.String()
}

// ExprStmt represents an expression statement.
type ExprStmt struct {
	Expr Expr
}

func (s *ExprStmt) stmtNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (s *ExprStmt) Pos() Pos {
	return s.Expr.Pos()
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (s *ExprStmt) End() Pos {
	return s.Expr.End()
}

func (s *ExprStmt) String() string {
	return s.Expr.String()
}

// ForInStmt represents a for-in statement.
type ForInStmt struct {
	ForPos   Pos
	Key      *Ident
	Value    *Ident
	Iterable Expr
	Body     *BlockStmt
}

func (s *ForInStmt) stmtNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (s *ForInStmt) Pos() Pos {
	return s.ForPos
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (s *ForInStmt) End() Pos {
	return s.Body.End()
}

func (s *ForInStmt) String() string {
	if s.Value != nil {
		return "for " + s.Key.String() + ", " + s.Value.String() +
			" in " + s.Iterable.String() + " " + s.Body.String()
	}
	return "for " + s.Key.String() + " in " + s.Iterable.String() +
		" " + s.Body.String()
}

// ForStmt represents a for statement.
type ForStmt struct {
	ForPos Pos
	Init   Stmt
	Cond   Expr
	Post   Stmt
	Body   *BlockStmt
}

func (s *ForStmt) stmtNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (s *ForStmt) Pos() Pos {
	return s.ForPos
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (s *ForStmt) End() Pos {
	return s.Body.End()
}

func (s *ForStmt) String() string {
	var init, cond, post string
	if s.Init != nil {
		init = s.Init.String()
	}
	if s.Cond != nil {
		cond = s.Cond.String() + " "
	}
	if s.Post != nil {
		post = s.Post.String()
	}

	if init != "" || post != "" {
		return "for " + init + " ; " + cond + " ; " + post + s.Body.String()
	}
	return "for " + cond + s.Body.String()
}

// IfStmt represents an if statement.
type IfStmt struct {
	IfPos Pos
	Init  Stmt
	Cond  Expr
	Body  *BlockStmt
	Else  Stmt // else branch; or nil
}

func (s *IfStmt) stmtNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (s *IfStmt) Pos() Pos {
	return s.IfPos
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (s *IfStmt) End() Pos {
	if s.Else != nil {
		return s.Else.End()
	}
	return s.Body.End()
}

func (s *IfStmt) String() string {
	var initStmt, elseStmt string
	if s.Init != nil {
		initStmt = s.Init.String() + "; "
	}
	if s.Else != nil {
		elseStmt = " else " + s.Else.String()
	}
	return "if " + initStmt + s.Cond.String() + " " +
		s.Body.String() + elseStmt
}

// IncDecStmt represents increment or decrement statement.
type IncDecStmt struct {
	Expr     Expr
	Token    token.Token
	TokenPos Pos
}

func (s *IncDecStmt) stmtNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (s *IncDecStmt) Pos() Pos {
	return s.Expr.Pos()
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (s *IncDecStmt) End() Pos {
	return Pos(int(s.TokenPos) + 2)
}

func (s *IncDecStmt) String() string {
	return s.Expr.String() + s.Token.String()
}

// ReturnStmt represents a return statement.
type ReturnStmt struct {
	ReturnPos Pos
	Result    Expr
}

func (s *ReturnStmt) stmtNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (s *ReturnStmt) Pos() Pos {
	return s.ReturnPos
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (s *ReturnStmt) End() Pos {
	if s.Result != nil {
		return s.Result.End()
	}
	return s.ReturnPos + 6
}

func (s *ReturnStmt) String() string {
	if s.Result != nil {
		return "return " + s.Result.String()
	}
	return "return"
}

   232	func (p *Parser) parsePrimaryExpr() Expr {
   233		if p.trace {
   234			defer untracep(tracep(p, "PrimaryExpression"))
   235		}
   236	
   237		x := p.parseOperand()
   238	
   239	L:
   240		for {
   241			switch p.token {
   242			case token.Period:
   243				p.next()
   244	
   245				switch p.token {
   246				case token.Ident:
   247					x = p.parseSelector(x)
   248				default:
   249					pos := p.pos
   250					p.errorExpected(pos, "selector")
   251					p.advance(stmtStart)
   252					return &BadExpr{From: pos, To: p.pos}
   253				}
   254			case token.LBrack:

   265	func (p *Parser) parseCall(x Expr) *CallExpr {
   266		if p.trace {
   267			defer untracep(tracep(p, "Call"))
   268		}
   269	
   270		lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
   271		p.exprLevel++
   272	
   273		var list []Expr
   274		var ellipsis Pos
   275		for p.token != token.RParen && p.token != token.EOF && !ellipsis.IsValid() {
   276			list = append(list, p.parseExpr())
   277			if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
   278				ellipsis = p.pos
   279				p.next()
   280			}
   281			if !p.expectComma(token.RParen, "call argument") {
   282				break
   283			}
   284		}
   285	
   286		p.exprLevel--
   287		rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
   288		return &CallExpr{
   289			Func:     x,
   290			LParen:   lparen,
   291			RParen:   rparen,
   292			Ellipsis: ellipsis,
   293			Args:     list,
   294		}
   295	}

   297	func (p *Parser) expectComma(closing token.Token, want string) bool {
   298		if p.token == token.Comma {
   299			p.next()
   300	
   301			if p.token == closing {
   302				p.errorExpected(p.pos, want)
   303				return false
   304			}
   305			return true
   306		}
   307	
   308		if p.token == token.Semicolon && p.tokenLit == "\n" {
   309			p.next()
   310		}
   311		return false
   312	}
   313	
   314	func (p *Parser) parseIndexOrSlice(x Expr) Expr {
   315		if p.trace {
   316			defer untracep(tracep(p, "IndexOrSlice"))
   317		}
   318	
   319		lbrack := p.expect(token.LBrack)
   320		p.exprLevel++
   321	
   322		var index [2]Expr
   323		if p.token != token.Colon {
   324			index[0] = p.parseExpr()
   325		}
   326		numColons := 0
   327		if p.token == token.Colon {
   328			numColons++
   329			p.next()
   330	
   331			if p.token != token.RBrack && p.token != token.EOF {
   332				index[1] = p.parseExpr()
   333			}
   334		}
   335	
   336		p.exprLevel--
   337		rbrack := p.expect(token.RBrack)
   338	
   339		if numColons > 0 {
   340			// slice expression
   341			return &SliceExpr{
   342				Expr:   x,
   343				LBrack: lbrack,
   344				RBrack: rbrack,
   345				Low:    index[0],
   346				High:   index[1],
   347			}
   348		}
   349		return &IndexExpr{
   350			Expr:   x,
   351			LBrack: lbrack,
   352			RBrack: rbrack,
   353			Index:  index[0],
   354		}
   355	}
   356	
   357	func (p *Parser) parseSelector(x Expr) Expr {
   358		if p.trace {
   359			defer untracep(tracep(p, "Selector"))
   360		}
   361	
   362		sel := p.parseIdent()
   363		return &SelectorExpr{Expr: x, Sel: &StringLit{
   364			Value:    sel.Name,
   365			ValuePos: sel.NamePos,
   366			Literal:  sel.Name,
   367		}}
   368	}
   369	
   370	func (p *Parser) parseOperand() Expr {
   371		if p.trace {
   372			defer untracep(tracep(p, "Operand"))
   373		}
   374	
   375		switch p.token {
   376		case token.Ident:
   377			return p.parseIdent()
   378		case token.Int:
   379			v, err := strconv.ParseInt(p.tokenLit, 0, 64)
   380			if err == strconv.ErrRange {
   381				p.error(p.pos, "number out of range")
   382			} else if err != nil {
   383				p.error(p.pos, "invalid integer")
   384			}
   385			x := &IntLit{
   386				Value:    v,
   387				ValuePos: p.pos,
   388				Literal:  p.tokenLit,
   389			}
   390			p.next()
   391			return x
   392	
   393		case token.Float:
   394			v, err := strconv.ParseFloat(p.tokenLit, 64)
   395			if err == strconv.ErrRange {
   396				p.error(p.pos, "number out of range")
   397			} else if err != nil {
   398				p.error(p.pos, "invalid float")
   399			}
   400			x := &FloatLit{
   401				Value:    v,
   402				ValuePos: p.pos,
   403				Literal:  p.tokenLit,
   404			}
   405			p.next()
   406			return x
   407		case token.Char:
   408			return p.parseCharLit()
   409		case token.String:
   410			v, _ := strconv.Unquote(p.tokenLit)
   411			x := &StringLit{
   412				Value:    v,
   413				ValuePos: p.pos,
   414				Literal:  p.tokenLit,
   415			}
   416			p.next()
   417			return x
   418		case token.True:
   419			x := &BoolLit{
   420				Value:    true,
   421				ValuePos: p.pos,
   422				Literal:  p.tokenLit,
   423			}
   424			p.next()
   425			return x
   426		case token.False:
   427			x := &BoolLit{
   428				Value:    false,
   429				ValuePos: p.pos,
   430				Literal:  p.tokenLit,
   431			}
   432			p.next()
   433			return x
   434		case token.Undefined:
   435			x := &UndefinedLit{TokenPos: p.pos}
   436			p.next()
   437			return x
   438		case token.Import:
   439			return p.parseImportExpr()
   440		case token.LParen:
   441			lparen := p.pos
   442			p.next()
   443			p.exprLevel++
   444			x := p.parseExpr()
   445			p.exprLevel--
   446			rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
   447			return &ParenExpr{
   448				LParen: lparen,
   449				Expr:   x,
   450				RParen: rparen,
   451			}
   452		case token.LBrack: // array literal
   453			return p.parseArrayLit()
   454		case token.LBrace: // map literal
   455			return p.parseMapLit()
   456		case token.Func: // function literal
   457			return p.parseFuncLit()
   458		case token.Error: // error expression
   459			return p.parseErrorExpr()
   460		case token.Immutable: // immutable expression
   461			return p.parseImmutableExpr()
   462		default:
   463			p.errorExpected(p.pos, "operand")
   464		}
   465	
   466		pos := p.pos
   467		p.advance(stmtStart)
   468		return &BadExpr{From: pos, To: p.pos}
   469	}
   470	
   471	func (p *Parser) parseImportExpr() Expr {
   472		pos := p.pos
   473		p.next()
   474		p.expect(token.LParen)
   475		if p.token != token.String {
   476			p.errorExpected(p.pos, "module name")
   477			p.advance(stmtStart)
   478			return &BadExpr{From: pos, To: p.pos}
   479		}
   480	
   481		// module name
   482		moduleName, _ := strconv.Unquote(p.tokenLit)
   483		expr := &ImportExpr{
   484			ModuleName: moduleName,
   485			Token:      token.Import,
   486			TokenPos:   pos,
   487		}
   488	
   489		p.next()
   490		p.expect(token.RParen)
   491		return expr
   492	}
   493	
   494	func (p *Parser) parseCharLit() Expr {
   495		if n := len(p.tokenLit); n >= 3 {
   496			code, _, _, err := strconv.UnquoteChar(p.tokenLit[1:n-1], '\'')
   497			if err == nil {
   498				x := &CharLit{
   499					Value:    code,
   500					ValuePos: p.pos,
   501					Literal:  p.tokenLit,
   502				}
   503				p.next()
   504				return x
   505			}
   506		}
   507	
   508		pos := p.pos
   509		p.error(pos, "illegal char literal")
   510		p.next()
   511		return &BadExpr{
   512			From: pos,
   513			To:   p.pos,
   514		}
   515	}
   516	
   517	func (p *Parser) parseFuncLit() Expr {
   518		if p.trace {
   519			defer untracep(tracep(p, "FuncLit"))
   520		}
   521	
   522		typ := p.parseFuncType()
   523		p.exprLevel++
   524		body := p.parseBody()
   525		p.exprLevel--
   526		return &FuncLit{
   527			Type: typ,
   528			Body: body,
   529		}
   530	}
   531	
   532	func (p *Parser) parseArrayLit() Expr {
   533		if p.trace {
   534			defer untracep(tracep(p, "ArrayLit"))
   535		}
   536	
   537		lbrack := p.expect(token.LBrack)
   538		p.exprLevel++
   539	
   540		var elements []Expr
   541		for p.token != token.RBrack && p.token != token.EOF {
   542			elements = append(elements, p.parseExpr())
   543	
   544			if !p.expectComma(token.RBrack, "array element") {
   545				break
   546			}
   547		}
   548	
   549		p.exprLevel--
   550		rbrack := p.expect(token.RBrack)
   551		return &ArrayLit{
   552			Elements: elements,
   553			LBrack:   lbrack,
   554			RBrack:   rbrack,
   555		}
   556	}
   557	
   558	func (p *Parser) parseErrorExpr() Expr {
   559		pos := p.pos
   560	
   561		p.next()
   562		lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
   563		value := p.parseExpr()
   564		rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
   565		return &ErrorExpr{
   566			ErrorPos: pos,
   567			Expr:     value,
   568			LParen:   lparen,
   569			RParen:   rparen,
   570		}
   571	}
   572	
   573	func (p *Parser) parseImmutableExpr() Expr {
   574		pos := p.pos
   575	
   576		p.next()
   577		lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
   578		value := p.parseExpr()
   579		rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
   580		return &ImmutableExpr{
   581			ErrorPos: pos,
   582			Expr:     value,
   583			LParen:   lparen,
   584			RParen:   rparen,
   585		}
   586	}
   587	
   588	func (p *Parser) parseFuncType() *FuncType {
   589		if p.trace {
   590			defer untracep(tracep(p, "FuncType"))
   591		}
   592	
   593		pos := p.expect(token.Func)
   594		params := p.parseIdentList()
   595		return &FuncType{
   596			FuncPos: pos,
   597			Params:  params,
   598		}
   599	}
   600	
   601	func (p *Parser) parseBody() *BlockStmt {
   602		if p.trace {
   603			defer untracep(tracep(p, "Body"))
   604		}
   605	
   606		lbrace := p.expect(token.LBrace)
   607		list := p.parseStmtList()
   608		rbrace := p.expect(token.RBrace)
   609		return &BlockStmt{
   610			LBrace: lbrace,
   611			RBrace: rbrace,
   612			Stmts:  list,
   613		}
   614	}

   617		if p.trace {
   618			defer untracep(tracep(p, "StatementList"))
   619		}

   616	func (p *Parser) parseStmtList() (list []Stmt) {
   617		if p.trace {
   618			defer untracep(tracep(p, "StatementList"))
   619		}
   620	
   621		for p.token != token.RBrace && p.token != token.EOF {
   622			list = append(list, p.parseStmt())
   623		}
   624		return
   625	}
   626	
   627	func (p *Parser) parseIdent() *Ident {
   628		pos := p.pos
   629		name := "_"
   630	
   631		if p.token == token.Ident {
   632			name = p.tokenLit
   633			p.next()
   634		} else {
   635			p.expect(token.Ident)
   636		}
   637		return &Ident{
   638			NamePos: pos,
   639			Name:    name,
   640		}
   641	}
   642	
   643	func (p *Parser) parseIdentList() *IdentList {
   644		if p.trace {
   645			defer untracep(tracep(p, "IdentList"))
   646		}
   647	
   648		var params []*Ident
   649		lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
   650		isVarArgs := false
   651		if p.token != token.RParen {
   652			if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
   653				isVarArgs = true
   654				p.next()
   655			}
   656	
   657			params = append(params, p.parseIdent())
   658			for !isVarArgs && p.token == token.Comma {
   659				p.next()
   660				if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
   661					isVarArgs = true
   662					p.next()
   663				}
   664				params = append(params, p.parseIdent())
   665			}
   666		}
   667	
   668		rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
   669		return &IdentList{
   670			LParen:  lparen,
   671			RParen:  rparen,
   672			VarArgs: isVarArgs,
   673			List:    params,
   674		}
   675	}
   676	
   677	func (p *Parser) parseStmt() (stmt Stmt) {
   678		if p.trace {
   679			defer untracep(tracep(p, "Statement"))
   680		}
   681	
   682		switch p.token {
   683		case // simple statements
   684			token.Func, token.Error, token.Immutable, token.Ident, token.Int,
   685			token.Float, token.Char, token.String, token.True, token.False,
   686			token.Undefined, token.Import, token.LParen, token.LBrace,
   687			token.LBrack, token.Add, token.Sub, token.Mul, token.And, token.Xor,
   688			token.Not:
   689			s := p.parseSimpleStmt(false)
   690			p.expectSemi()
   691			return s
   692		case token.Return:
   693			return p.parseReturnStmt()
   694		case token.Export:
   695			return p.parseExportStmt()
   696		case token.If:
   697			return p.parseIfStmt()
   698		case token.For:
   699			return p.parseForStmt()
   700		case token.Break, token.Continue:
   701			return p.parseBranchStmt(p.token)
   702		case token.Semicolon:
   703			s := &EmptyStmt{Semicolon: p.pos, Implicit: p.tokenLit == "\n"}
   704			p.next()
   705			return s
   706		case token.RBrace:
   707			// semicolon may be omitted before a closing "}"
   708			return &EmptyStmt{Semicolon: p.pos, Implicit: true}
   709		default:
   710			pos := p.pos
   711			p.errorExpected(pos, "statement")
   712			p.advance(stmtStart)
   713			return &BadStmt{From: pos, To: p.pos}
   714		}
   715	}
   716	
   717	func (p *Parser) parseForStmt() Stmt {
   718		if p.trace {
   719			defer untracep(tracep(p, "ForStmt"))
   720		}
   721	
   722		pos := p.expect(token.For)
   723	
   724		// for {}
   725		if p.token == token.LBrace {
   726			body := p.parseBlockStmt()
   727			p.expectSemi()
   728	
   729			return &ForStmt{
   730				ForPos: pos,
   731				Body:   body,
   732			}
   733		}
   734	
   735		prevLevel := p.exprLevel
   736		p.exprLevel = -1
   737	
   738		var s1 Stmt
   739		if p.token != token.Semicolon { // skipping init
   740			s1 = p.parseSimpleStmt(true)
   741		}
   742	
   743		// for _ in seq {}            or
   744		// for value in seq {}        or
   745		// for key, value in seq {}
   746		if forInStmt, isForIn := s1.(*ForInStmt); isForIn {
   747			forInStmt.ForPos = pos
   748			p.exprLevel = prevLevel
   749			forInStmt.Body = p.parseBlockStmt()
   750			p.expectSemi()
   751			return forInStmt
   752		}
   753	
   754		// for init; cond; post {}
   755		var s2, s3 Stmt
   756		if p.token == token.Semicolon {
   757			p.next()
   758			if p.token != token.Semicolon {
   759				s2 = p.parseSimpleStmt(false) // cond
   760			}
   761			p.expect(token.Semicolon)
   762			if p.token != token.LBrace {
   763				s3 = p.parseSimpleStmt(false) // post
   764			}
   765		} else {
   766			// for cond {}
   767			s2 = s1
   768			s1 = nil
   769		}
   770	
   771		// body
   772		p.exprLevel = prevLevel
   773		body := p.parseBlockStmt()
   774		p.expectSemi()
   775		cond := p.makeExpr(s2, "condition expression")
   776		return &ForStmt{
   777			ForPos: pos,
   778			Init:   s1,
   779			Cond:   cond,
   780			Post:   s3,
   781			Body:   body,
   782		}
   783	}
   784	
   785	func (p *Parser) parseBranchStmt(tok token.Token) Stmt {
   786		if p.trace {
   787			defer untracep(tracep(p, "BranchStmt"))
   788		}
   789	
   790		pos := p.expect(tok)
   791	
   792		var label *Ident
   793		if p.token == token.Ident {
   794			label = p.parseIdent()
   795		}
   796		p.expectSemi()
   797		return &BranchStmt{
   798			Token:    tok,
   799			TokenPos: pos,
   800			Label:    label,
   801		}
   802	}
   803	
   804	func (p *Parser) parseIfStmt() Stmt {
   805		if p.trace {
   806			defer untracep(tracep(p, "IfStmt"))
   807		}
   808	
   809		pos := p.expect(token.If)
   810		init, cond := p.parseIfHeader()
   811		body := p.parseBlockStmt()
   812	
   813		var elseStmt Stmt
   814		if p.token == token.Else {
   815			p.next()
   816	
   817			switch p.token {
   818			case token.If:
   819				elseStmt = p.parseIfStmt()
   820			case token.LBrace:
   821				elseStmt = p.parseBlockStmt()
   822				p.expectSemi()
   823			default:
   824				p.errorExpected(p.pos, "if or {")
   825				elseStmt = &BadStmt{From: p.pos, To: p.pos}
   826			}
   827		} else {
   828			p.expectSemi()
   829		}
   830		return &IfStmt{
   831			IfPos: pos,
   832			Init:  init,
   833			Cond:  cond,
   834			Body:  body,
   835			Else:  elseStmt,
   836		}
   837	}
   838	
   839	func (p *Parser) parseBlockStmt() *BlockStmt {
   840		if p.trace {
   841			defer untracep(tracep(p, "BlockStmt"))
   842		}
   843	
   844		lbrace := p.expect(token.LBrace)
   845		list := p.parseStmtList()
   846		rbrace := p.expect(token.RBrace)
   847		return &BlockStmt{
   848			LBrace: lbrace,
   849			RBrace: rbrace,
   850			Stmts:  list,
   851		}
   852	}
   853	
   854	func (p *Parser) parseIfHeader() (init Stmt, cond Expr) {
   855		if p.token == token.LBrace {
   856			p.error(p.pos, "missing condition in if statement")
   857			cond = &BadExpr{From: p.pos, To: p.pos}
   858			return
   859		}
   860	
   861		outer := p.exprLevel
   862		p.exprLevel = -1
   863		if p.token == token.Semicolon {
   864			p.error(p.pos, "missing init in if statement")
   865			return
   866		}
   867		init = p.parseSimpleStmt(false)
   868	
   869		var condStmt Stmt
   870		if p.token == token.LBrace {
   871			condStmt = init
   872			init = nil
   873		} else if p.token == token.Semicolon {
   874			p.next()
   875	
   876			condStmt = p.parseSimpleStmt(false)
   877		} else {
   878			p.error(p.pos, "missing condition in if statement")
   879		}
   880	
   881		if condStmt != nil {
   882			cond = p.makeExpr(condStmt, "boolean expression")
   883		}
   884		if cond == nil {
   885			cond = &BadExpr{From: p.pos, To: p.pos}
   886		}
   887		p.exprLevel = outer
   888		return
   889	}
   890	
   891	func (p *Parser) makeExpr(s Stmt, want string) Expr {
   892		if s == nil {
   893			return nil
   894		}
   895	
   896		if es, isExpr := s.(*ExprStmt); isExpr {
   897			return es.Expr
   898		}
   899	
   900		found := "simple statement"

   273			return c.compileForStmt(node)
   274		case *parser.ForInStmt:
   275			return c.compileForInStmt(node)
   276		case *parser.BranchStmt:

   278				curLoop := c.currentLoop()
   279				if curLoop == nil {
   280					return c.errorf(node, "break not allowed outside loop")
   281				}
   282				pos := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
   283				curLoop.Breaks = append(curLoop.Breaks, pos)
   284			} else if node.Token == token.Continue {
   285				curLoop := c.currentLoop()
   286				if curLoop == nil {
   287					return c.errorf(node, "continue not allowed outside loop")
   288				}
   289				pos := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
   290				curLoop.Continues = append(curLoop.Continues, pos)
   291			} else {
   292				panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid branch statement: %s",
   293					node.Token.String()))
   294			}
   295		case *parser.BlockStmt:
   296			if len(node.Stmts) == 0 {
   297				return nil
   298			}
   299	
   300			c.symbolTable = c.symbolTable.Fork(true)
   301			defer func() {
   302				c.symbolTable = c.symbolTable.Parent(false)
   303			}()
   304	
   305			for _, stmt := range node.Stmts {
   306				if err := c.Compile(stmt); err != nil {
   307					return err
   308				}
   309			}
   310		case *parser.AssignStmt:
   311			err := c.compileAssign(node, node.LHS, node.RHS, node.Token)
   312			if err != nil {
   313				return err
   314			}
   315		case *parser.Ident:
   316			symbol, _, ok := c.symbolTable.Resolve(node.Name, false)
   317			if !ok {
   318				return c.errorf(node, "unresolved reference '%s'", node.Name)
   319			}
   320	
   321			switch symbol.Scope {
   322			case ScopeGlobal:
   323				c.emit(node, parser.OpGetGlobal, symbol.Index)
   324			case ScopeLocal:
   325				c.emit(node, parser.OpGetLocal, symbol.Index)
   326			case ScopeBuiltin:
   327				c.emit(node, parser.OpGetBuiltin, symbol.Index)
   328			case ScopeFree:
   329				c.emit(node, parser.OpGetFree, symbol.Index)
   330			}
   331		case *parser.ArrayLit:
   332			for _, elem := range node.Elements {
   333				if err := c.Compile(elem); err != nil {
   334					return err
   335				}
   336			}
   337			c.emit(node, parser.OpArray, len(node.Elements))
   338		case *parser.MapLit:
   339			for _, elt := range node.Elements {
   340				// key
   341				if len(elt.Key) > MaxStringLen {
   342					return c.error(node, ErrStringLimit)
   343				}
   344				c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant,
   345					c.addConstant(&String{Value: elt.Key}))
   346	
   347				// value
   348				if err := c.Compile(elt.Value); err != nil {
   349					return err
   350				}
   351			}
   352			c.emit(node, parser.OpMap, len(node.Elements)*2)
   353	
   354		case *parser.SelectorExpr: // selector on RHS side
   355			if err := c.Compile(node.Expr); err != nil {
   356				return err
   357			}
   358			if err := c.Compile(node.Sel); err != nil {
   359				return err
   360			}
   361			c.emit(node, parser.OpIndex)
   362		case *parser.IndexExpr:
   363			if err := c.Compile(node.Expr); err != nil {
   364				return err
   365			}
   366			if err := c.Compile(node.Index); err != nil {
   367				return err
   368			}
   369			c.emit(node, parser.OpIndex)
   370		case *parser.SliceExpr:
   371			if err := c.Compile(node.Expr); err != nil {
   372				return err
   373			}
   374			if node.Low != nil {
   375				if err := c.Compile(node.Low); err != nil {
   376					return err
   377				}
   378			} else {
   379				c.emit(node, parser.OpNull)
   380			}
   381			if node.High != nil {
   382				if err := c.Compile(node.High); err != nil {
   383					return err
   384				}
   385			} else {
   386				c.emit(node, parser.OpNull)
   387			}
   388			c.emit(node, parser.OpSliceIndex)
   389		case *parser.FuncLit:
   390			c.enterScope()
   391	
   392			for _, p := range node.Type.Params.List {
   393				s := c.symbolTable.Define(p.Name)
   394	
   395				// function arguments is not assigned directly.
   396				s.LocalAssigned = true
   397			}
   398	
   399			if err := c.Compile(node.Body); err != nil {
   400				return err
   401			}
   402	
   403			// code optimization
   404			c.optimizeFunc(node)
   405	
   406			freeSymbols := c.symbolTable.FreeSymbols()
   407			numLocals := c.symbolTable.MaxSymbols()
   408			instructions, sourceMap := c.leaveScope()
   409	
   410			for _, s := range freeSymbols {
   411				switch s.Scope {
   412				case ScopeLocal:
   413					if !s.LocalAssigned {
   414						// Here, the closure is capturing a local variable that's
   415						// not yet assigned its value. One example is a local
   416						// recursive function:
   417						//
   418						//   func() {
   419						//     foo := func(x) {
   420						//       // ..
===== 680-760 =====
   680	
   681		_, isFunc := rhs[0].(*parser.FuncLit)
   682		symbol, depth, exists := c.symbolTable.Resolve(ident, false)
   683		if op == token.Define {
   684			if depth == 0 && exists {
   685				return c.errorf(node, "'%s' redeclared in this block", ident)
   686			}
   687			if isFunc {
   688				symbol = c.symbolTable.Define(ident)
   689			}
   690		} else {
   691			if !exists {
   692				return c.errorf(node, "unresolved reference '%s'", ident)
   693			}
   694		}
   695	
   696		// +=, -=, *=, /=
   697		if op != token.Assign && op != token.Define {
   698			if err := c.Compile(lhs[0]); err != nil {
   699				return err
   700			}
   701		}
   702	
   703		// compile RHSs
   704		for _, expr := range rhs {
   705			if err := c.Compile(expr); err != nil {
   706				return err
   707			}
   708		}
   709	
   710		if op == token.Define && !isFunc {
   711			symbol = c.symbolTable.Define(ident)
   712		}
   713	
   714		switch op {
   715		case token.AddAssign:
   716			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Add))
   717		case token.SubAssign:
   718			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Sub))
   719		case token.MulAssign:
   720			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Mul))
   721		case token.QuoAssign:
   722			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Quo))
   723		case token.RemAssign:
   724			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Rem))
   725		case token.AndAssign:
   726			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.And))
   727		case token.OrAssign:
   728			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Or))
   729		case token.AndNotAssign:
   730			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.AndNot))
   731		case token.XorAssign:
   732			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Xor))
   733		case token.ShlAssign:
   734			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Shl))
   735		case token.ShrAssign:
   736			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Shr))
   737		}
   738	
   739		// compile selector expressions (right to left)
   740		for i := numSel - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   741			if err := c.Compile(selectors[i]); err != nil {
   742				return err
   743			}
   744		}
   745	
   746		switch symbol.Scope {
   747		case ScopeGlobal:
   748			if numSel > 0 {
   749				c.emit(node, parser.OpSetSelGlobal, symbol.Index, numSel)
   750			} else {
   751				c.emit(node, parser.OpSetGlobal, symbol.Index)
   752			}
   753		case ScopeLocal:
   754			if numSel > 0 {
   755				c.emit(node, parser.OpSetSelLocal, symbol.Index, numSel)
   756			} else {
   757				if op == token.Define && !symbol.LocalAssigned {
   758					c.emit(node, parser.OpDefineLocal, symbol.Index)
   759				} else {
   760					c.emit(node, parser.OpSetLocal, symbol.Index)
   761				}
   762			}
   763	
   764			// mark the symbol as local-assigned
   765			symbol.LocalAssigned = true
   766		case ScopeFree:
   767			if numSel > 0 {
   768				c.emit(node, parser.OpSetSelFree, symbol.Index, numSel)
   769			} else {
   770				c.emit(node, parser.OpSetFree, symbol.Index)
   771			}
   772		default:
   773			panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid assignment variable scope: %s",
   774				symbol.Scope))
   775		}
   776		return nil
   777	}

   780		// left side term

   629	// SetImportFileExt sets the extension name of the source file for loading
   630	// local module files.
   631	//
   632	// Use this method if you want other source file extension than ".tengo".
   633	//
   634	//     // this will search for *.tengo, *.foo, *.bar
   635	//     err := c.SetImportFileExt(".tengo", ".foo", ".bar")
   636	//
   637	// This function requires at least one argument, since it will replace the
   638	// current list of extension name.
   639	func (c *Compiler) SetImportFileExt(exts ...string) error {
   640		if len(exts) == 0 {
   641			return fmt.Errorf("missing arg: at least one argument is required")
   642		}
   643	
   644		for _, ext := range exts {
   645			if ext != filepath.Ext(ext) || ext == "" {
   646				return fmt.Errorf("invalid file extension: %s", ext)
   647			}
   648		}
   649	
   650		c.importFileExt = exts // Replace the hole current extension list
   651	
   652		return nil
   653	}
   654	
   655	// GetImportFileExt returns the current list of extension name.
   656	// Thease are the complementary suffix of the source file to search and load
   657	// local module files.
   658	func (c *Compiler) GetImportFileExt() []string {
   659		return c.importFileExt
   660	}

   662	func (c *Compiler) compileAssign(
   663		node parser.Node,
   664		lhs, rhs []parser.Expr,
   665		op token.Token,
   666	) error {
   667		numLHS, numRHS := len(lhs), len(rhs)
   668		if numLHS > 1 || numRHS > 1 {
   669			return c.errorf(node, "tuple assignment not allowed")
   670		}
   671	
   672		// resolve and compile left-hand side
   673		ident, selectors := resolveAssignLHS(lhs[0])
   674		numSel := len(selectors)
   675	
   676		if op == token.Define && numSel > 0 {
   677			// using selector on new variable does not make sense
   678			return c.errorf(node, "operator ':=' not allowed with selector")
   679		}
   680	
   681		_, isFunc := rhs[0].(*parser.FuncLit)
   682		symbol, depth, exists := c.symbolTable.Resolve(ident, false)
   683		if op == token.Define {
   684			if depth == 0 && exists {
   685				return c.errorf(node, "'%s' redeclared in this block", ident)
   686			}
   687			if isFunc {
   688				symbol = c.symbolTable.Define(ident)
   689			}
   690		} else {
   691			if !exists {
   692				return c.errorf(node, "unresolved reference '%s'", ident)
   693			}
   694		}
   695	
   696		// +=, -=, *=, /=
   697		if op != token.Assign && op != token.Define {
   698			if err := c.Compile(lhs[0]); err != nil {
   699				return err
   700			}
   701		}
   702	
   703		// compile RHSs
   704		for _, expr := range rhs {
   705			if err := c.Compile(expr); err != nil {
   706				return err
   707			}
   708		}
   709	
   710		if op == token.Define && !isFunc {
   711			symbol = c.symbolTable.Define(ident)
   712		}
   713	
   714		switch op {
   715		case token.AddAssign:
   716			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Add))
   717		case token.SubAssign:
   718			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Sub))
   719		case token.MulAssign:
   720			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Mul))
   721		case token.QuoAssign:
   722			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Quo))
   723		case token.RemAssign:
   724			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Rem))
   725		case token.AndAssign:
   726			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.And))
   727		case token.OrAssign:
   728			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Or))
   729		case token.AndNotAssign:
   730			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.AndNot))
   731		case token.XorAssign:
   732			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Xor))
   733		case token.ShlAssign:
   734			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Shl))
   735		case token.ShrAssign:
   736			c.emit(node, parser.OpBinaryOp, int(token.Shr))
   737		}
   738	
   739		// compile selector expressions (right to left)
   740		for i := numSel - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   741			if err := c.Compile(selectors[i]); err != nil {
   742				return err
   743			}
   744		}
   745	
   746		switch symbol.Scope {
   747		case ScopeGlobal:
   748			if numSel > 0 {
   749				c.emit(node, parser.OpSetSelGlobal, symbol.Index, numSel)
   750			} else {
   751				c.emit(node, parser.OpSetGlobal, symbol.Index)
   752			}
   753		case ScopeLocal:
   754			if numSel > 0 {
   755				c.emit(node, parser.OpSetSelLocal, symbol.Index, numSel)
   756			} else {
   757				if op == token.Define && !symbol.LocalAssigned {
   758					c.emit(node, parser.OpDefineLocal, symbol.Index)
   759				} else {
   760					c.emit(node, parser.OpSetLocal, symbol.Index)
   761				}
   762			}
   763	
   764			// mark the symbol as local-assigned
   765			symbol.LocalAssigned = true
   766		case ScopeFree:
   767			if numSel > 0 {
   768				c.emit(node, parser.OpSetSelFree, symbol.Index, numSel)
   769			} else {
   770				c.emit(node, parser.OpSetFree, symbol.Index)
   771			}
   772		default:
   773			panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid assignment variable scope: %s",
   774				symbol.Scope))
   775		}
   776		return nil
   777	}

   779	func (c *Compiler) compileLogical(node *parser.BinaryExpr) error {
   780		// left side term
   781		if err := c.Compile(node.LHS); err != nil {
   782			return err
   783		}
   784	
   785		// jump position
   786		var jumpPos int
   787		if node.Token == token.LAnd {
   788			jumpPos = c.emit(node, parser.OpAndJump, 0)
   789		} else {
   790			jumpPos = c.emit(node, parser.OpOrJump, 0)
   791		}
   792	
   793		// right side term
   794		if err := c.Compile(node.RHS); err != nil {
   795			return err
   796		}
   797	
   798		c.changeOperand(jumpPos, len(c.currentInstructions()))
   799		return nil
   800	}
   801	
   802	func (c *Compiler) compileForStmt(stmt *parser.ForStmt) error {
   803		c.symbolTable = c.symbolTable.Fork(true)
   804		defer func() {
   805			c.symbolTable = c.symbolTable.Parent(false)
   806		}()
   807	
   808		// init statement
   809		if stmt.Init != nil {
   810			if err := c.Compile(stmt.Init); err != nil {
   811				return err
   812			}
   813		}
   814	
   815		// pre-condition position
   816		preCondPos := len(c.currentInstructions())
   817	
   818		// condition expression
   819		postCondPos := -1
   820		if stmt.Cond != nil {
   821			if err := c.Compile(stmt.Cond); err != nil {
   822				return err
   823			}
   824			// condition jump position
   825			postCondPos = c.emit(stmt, parser.OpJumpFalsy, 0)
   826		}
   827	
   828		// enter loop
   829		loop := c.enterLoop()
   830	
   831		// body statement
   832		if err := c.Compile(stmt.Body); err != nil {
   833			c.leaveLoop()
   834			return err
   835		}
   836	
   837		c.leaveLoop()
   838	
   839		// post-body position
   840		postBodyPos := len(c.currentInstructions())
   841	
   842		// post statement
   843		if stmt.Post != nil {
   844			if err := c.Compile(stmt.Post); err != nil {
   845				return err
   846			}
   847		}
   848	
   849		// back to condition
   850		c.emit(stmt, parser.OpJump, preCondPos)
   851	
   852		// post-statement position
   853		postStmtPos := len(c.currentInstructions())
   854		if postCondPos >= 0 {
   855			c.changeOperand(postCondPos, postStmtPos)
   856		}
   857	
   858		// update all break/continue jump positions
   859		for _, pos := range loop.Breaks {
   860			c.changeOperand(pos, postStmtPos)
==== opcodes ====
package parser

// Opcode represents a single byte operation code.
type Opcode = byte

// List of opcodes
const (
	OpConstant      Opcode = iota // Load constant
	OpBComplement                 // bitwise complement
	OpPop                         // Pop
	OpTrue                        // Push true
	OpFalse                       // Push false
	OpEqual                       // Equal ==
	OpNotEqual                    // Not equal !=
	OpMinus                       // Minus -
	OpLNot                        // Logical not !
	OpJumpFalsy                   // Jump if falsy
	OpAndJump                     // Logical AND jump
	OpOrJump                      // Logical OR jump
	OpJump                        // Jump
	OpNull                        // Push null
	OpArray                       // Array object
	OpMap                         // Map object
	OpError                       // Error object
	OpImmutable                   // Immutable object
	OpIndex                       // Index operation
	OpSliceIndex                  // Slice operation
	OpCall                        // Call function
	OpReturn                      // Return
	OpGetGlobal                   // Get global variable
	OpSetGlobal                   // Set global variable
	OpSetSelGlobal                // Set global variable using selectors
	OpGetLocal                    // Get local variable
	OpSetLocal                    // Set local variable
	OpDefineLocal                 // Define local variable
	OpSetSelLocal                 // Set local variable using selectors
	OpGetFreePtr                  // Get free variable pointer object
	OpGetFree                     // Get free variables
	OpSetFree                     // Set free variables
	OpGetLocalPtr                 // Get local variable as a pointer
	OpSetSelFree                  // Set free variables using selectors
	OpGetBuiltin                  // Get builtin function
	OpClosure                     // Push closure
	OpIteratorInit                // Iterator init
	OpIteratorNext                // Iterator next
	OpIteratorKey                 // Iterator key
	OpIteratorValue               // Iterator value
	OpBinaryOp                    // Binary operation
	OpSuspend                     // Suspend VM
)

// OpcodeNames are string representation of opcodes.
var OpcodeNames = [...]string{
	OpConstant:      "CONST",
	OpPop:           "POP",
	OpTrue:          "TRUE",
	OpFalse:         "FALSE",
	OpBComplement:   "NEG",
	OpEqual:         "EQL",
	OpNotEqual:      "NEQ",
	OpMinus:         "NEG",
	OpLNot:          "NOT",
	OpJumpFalsy:     "JMPF",
	OpAndJump:       "ANDJMP",
	OpOrJump:        "ORJMP",
	OpJump:          "JMP",
	OpNull:          "NULL",
	OpGetGlobal:     "GETG",
	OpSetGlobal:     "SETG",
	OpSetSelGlobal:  "SETSG",
	OpArray:         "ARR",
	OpMap:           "MAP",
	OpError:         "ERROR",
	OpImmutable:     "IMMUT",
	OpIndex:         "INDEX",
	OpSliceIndex:    "SLICE",
	OpCall:          "CALL",
	OpReturn:        "RET",
	OpGetLocal:      "GETL",
	OpSetLocal:      "SETL",
	OpDefineLocal:   "DEFL",
	OpSetSelLocal:   "SETSL",
	OpGetBuiltin:    "BUILTIN",
	OpClosure:       "CLOSURE",
	OpGetFreePtr:    "GETFP",
	OpGetFree:       "GETF",
	OpSetFree:       "SETF",
	OpGetLocalPtr:   "GETLP",
	OpSetSelFree:    "SETSF",
	OpIteratorInit:  "ITER",
	OpIteratorNext:  "ITNXT",
	OpIteratorKey:   "ITKEY",
	OpIteratorValue: "ITVAL",
	OpBinaryOp:      "BINARYOP",
	OpSuspend:       "SUSPEND",
}

// OpcodeOperands is the number of operands.
var OpcodeOperands = [...][]int{
	OpConstant:      {2},
	OpPop:           {},
	OpTrue:          {},
	OpFalse:         {},
	OpBComplement:   {},
	OpEqual:         {},
	OpNotEqual:      {},
	OpMinus:         {},
	OpLNot:          {},
	OpJumpFalsy:     {4},
	OpAndJump:       {4},
	OpOrJump:        {4},
	OpJump:          {4},
	OpNull:          {},
	OpGetGlobal:     {2},
	OpSetGlobal:     {2},
	OpSetSelGlobal:  {2, 1},
	OpArray:         {2},
	OpMap:           {2},
	OpError:         {},
	OpImmutable:     {},
	OpIndex:         {},
	OpSliceIndex:    {},
	OpCall:          {1, 1},
	OpReturn:        {1},
	OpGetLocal:      {1},
	OpSetLocal:      {1},
	OpDefineLocal:   {1},
	OpSetSelLocal:   {1, 1},
	OpGetBuiltin:    {1},
	OpClosure:       {2, 1},
	OpGetFreePtr:    {1},
	OpGetFree:       {1},
	OpSetFree:       {1},
	OpGetLocalPtr:   {1},
	OpSetSelFree:    {1, 1},
	OpIteratorInit:  {},
	OpIteratorNext:  {},
	OpIteratorKey:   {},
	OpIteratorValue: {},
	OpBinaryOp:      {1},
	OpSuspend:       {},
}

// ReadOperands reads operands from the bytecode.
func ReadOperands(numOperands []int, ins []byte) (operands []int, offset int) {
	for _, width := range numOperands {
		switch width {
		case 1:
			operands = append(operands, int(ins[offset]))
		case 2:
			operands = append(operands, int(ins[offset+1])|int(ins[offset])<<8)
		case 4:
			operands = append(operands, int(ins[offset+3])|int(ins[offset+2])<<8|int(ins[offset+1])<<16|int(ins[offset])<<24)
		}
		offset += width
	}
	return
}
==== instructions ====
package tengo

import (
	"fmt"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
)

// MakeInstruction returns a bytecode for an opcode and the operands.
func MakeInstruction(opcode parser.Opcode, operands ...int) []byte {
	numOperands := parser.OpcodeOperands[opcode]

	totalLen := 1
	for _, w := range numOperands {
		totalLen += w
	}

	instruction := make([]byte, totalLen)
	instruction[0] = opcode

	offset := 1
	for i, o := range operands {
		width := numOperands[i]
		switch width {
		case 1:
			instruction[offset] = byte(o)
		case 2:
			n := uint16(o)
			instruction[offset] = byte(n >> 8)
			instruction[offset+1] = byte(n)
		case 4:
			n := uint32(o)
			instruction[offset] = byte(n >> 24)
			instruction[offset+1] = byte(n >> 16)
			instruction[offset+2] = byte(n >> 8)
			instruction[offset+3] = byte(n)
		}
		offset += width
	}
	return instruction
}

// FormatInstructions returns string representation of bytecode instructions.
func FormatInstructions(b []byte, posOffset int) []string {
	var out []string

	i := 0
	for i < len(b) {
		numOperands := parser.OpcodeOperands[b[i]]
		operands, read := parser.ReadOperands(numOperands, b[i+1:])

		switch len(numOperands) {
		case 0:
			out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("%04d %-7s",
				posOffset+i, parser.OpcodeNames[b[i]]))
		case 1:
			out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("%04d %-7s %-5d",
				posOffset+i, parser.OpcodeNames[b[i]], operands[0]))
		case 2:
			out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("%04d %-7s %-5d %-5d",
				posOffset+i, parser.OpcodeNames[b[i]],
				operands[0], operands[1]))
		}
		i += 1 + read
	}
	return out
}

END STEP

    97	func (v *VM) run() {
    98		for atomic.LoadInt64(&v.aborting) == 0 {
    99			v.ip++
   100	
   101			switch v.curInsts[v.ip] {
   102			case parser.OpConstant:
   103				v.ip += 2
   104				cidx := int(v.curInsts[v.ip]) | int(v.curInsts[v.ip-1])<<8
   105	
   106				v.stack[v.sp] = v.constants[cidx]
   107				v.sp++
   108			case parser.OpNull:
   109				v.stack[v.sp] = UndefinedValue
   110				v.sp++
   111			case parser.OpBinaryOp:
   112				v.ip++
   113				right := v.stack[v.sp-1]
   114				left := v.stack[v.sp-2]
   115				tok := token.Token(v.curInsts[v.ip])
   116				res, e := left.BinaryOp(tok, right)
   117				if e != nil {
   118					v.sp -= 2
   119					if e == ErrInvalidOperator {
   120						v.err = fmt.Errorf("invalid operation: %s %s %s",
   121							left.TypeName(), tok.String(), right.TypeName())
   122						return
   123					}
   124					v.err = e
   125					return
   126				}
   127	
   128				v.allocs--
   129				if v.allocs == 0 {
   130					v.err = ErrObjectAllocLimit
   131					return
   132				}
   133	
   134				v.stack[v.sp-2] = res
   135				v.sp--
   136			case parser.OpEqual:
   137				right := v.stack[v.sp-1]
   138				left := v.stack[v.sp-2]
   139				v.sp -= 2
   140				if left.Equals(right) {
   141					v.stack[v.sp] = TrueValue
   142				} else {
   143					v.stack[v.sp] = FalseValue
   144				}
   145				v.sp++
   146			case parser.OpNotEqual:
   147				right := v.stack[v.sp-1]
   148				left := v.stack[v.sp-2]
   149				v.sp -= 2
   150				if left.Equals(right) {
   151					v.stack[v.sp] = FalseValue
   152				} else {
   153					v.stack[v.sp] = TrueValue
   154				}
   155				v.sp++
   156			case parser.OpPop:
   157				v.sp--
   158			case parser.OpTrue:
   159				v.stack[v.sp] = TrueValue
   160				v.sp++
   161			case parser.OpFalse:
   162				v.stack[v.sp] = FalseValue
   163				v.sp++
   164			case parser.OpLNot:
   165				operand := v.stack[v.sp-1]
   166				v.sp--
   167				if operand.IsFalsy() {
   168					v.stack[v.sp] = TrueValue
   169				} else {
   170					v.stack[v.sp] = FalseValue
   171				}
   172				v.sp++
   173			case parser.OpBComplement:
   174				operand := v.stack[v.sp-1]
   175				v.sp--
   176	
   177				switch x := operand.(type) {
   178				case *Int:

   183						return
   184					}

   186					v.sp++
   187				default:
   188					v.err = fmt.Errorf("invalid operation: ^%s",
   189						operand.TypeName())
   190					return
   191				}
   192			case parser.OpMinus:
   193				operand := v.stack[v.sp-1]
   194				v.sp--
   195	
   196				switch x := operand.(type) {
   197				case *Int:
   198					var res Object = &Int{Value: -x.Value}
   199					v.allocs--
   200					if v.allocs == 0 {
   201						v.err = ErrObjectAllocLimit
   202						return
   203					}
   204					v.stack[v.sp] = res
   205					v.sp++
   206				case *Float:
   207					var res Object = &Float{Value: -x.Value}
   208					v.allocs--
   209					if v.allocs == 0 {
   210						v.err = ErrObjectAllocLimit
   211						return
   212					}
   213					v.stack[v.sp] = res
   214					v.sp++
   215				default:
   216					v.err = fmt.Errorf("invalid operation: -%s",
   217						operand.TypeName())
   218					return
   219				}
   220			case parser.OpJumpFalsy:
   221				v.ip += 4
   222				v.sp--
   223				if v.stack[v.sp].IsFalsy() {
   224					pos := int(v.curInsts[v.ip]) | int(v.curInsts[v.ip-1])<<8 | int(v.curInsts[v.ip-2])<<16 | int(v.curInsts[v.ip-3])<<24
   225					v.ip = pos - 1

   540				}
   541			case parser.OpCall:
   542				numArgs := int(v.curInsts[v.ip+1])
   543				spread := int(v.curInsts[v.ip+2])
   544				v.ip += 2
   545	
   546				value := v.stack[v.sp-1-numArgs]
   547				if !value.CanCall() {
   548					v.err = fmt.Errorf("not callable: %s", value.TypeName())
   549					return
   550				}
   551	
   552				if spread == 1 {
   553					v.sp--
   554					switch arr := v.stack[v.sp].(type) {
   555					case *Array:
   556						for _, item := range arr.Value {
   557							v.stack[v.sp] = item
   558							v.sp++
   559						}
   560						numArgs += len(arr.Value) - 1
   561					case *ImmutableArray:
   562						for _, item := range arr.Value {
   563							v.stack[v.sp] = item
   564							v.sp++
   565						}
   566						numArgs += len(arr.Value) - 1
   567					default:
   568						v.err = fmt.Errorf("not an array: %s", arr.TypeName())
   569						return
   570					}
   571				}
   572	
   573				if callee, ok := value.(*CompiledFunction); ok {
   574					if callee.VarArgs {
   575						// if the closure is variadic,
   576						// roll up all variadic parameters into an array
   577						realArgs := callee.NumParameters - 1
   578						varArgs := numArgs - realArgs
   579						if varArgs >= 0 {
   580							numArgs = realArgs + 1
   581							args := make([]Object, varArgs)
   582							spStart := v.sp - varArgs
   583							for i := spStart; i < v.sp; i++ {
   584								args[i-spStart] = v.stack[i]
   585							}
   586							v.stack[spStart] = &Array{Value: args}
   587							v.sp = spStart + 1
   588						}
   589					}
   590					if numArgs != callee.NumParameters {
   591						if callee.VarArgs {
   592							v.err = fmt.Errorf(
   593								"wrong number of arguments: want>=%d, got=%d",
   594								callee.NumParameters-1, numArgs)
   595						} else {
   596							v.err = fmt.Errorf(
   597								"wrong number of arguments: want=%d, got=%d",
   598								callee.NumParameters, numArgs)
   599						}
   600						return
   601					}
   602	
   603					// test if it's tail-call
   604					if callee == v.curFrame.fn { // recursion
   605						nextOp := v.curInsts[v.ip+1]
   606						if nextOp == parser.OpReturn ||
   607							(nextOp == parser.OpPop &&
   608								parser.OpReturn == v.curInsts[v.ip+2]) {
   609							for p := 0; p < numArgs; p++ {
   610								v.stack[v.curFrame.basePointer+p] =
   611									v.stack[v.sp-numArgs+p]
   612							}
   613							v.sp -= numArgs + 1
   614							v.ip = -1 // reset IP to beginning of the frame
   615							continue
   616						}
   617					}
   618					if v.framesIndex >= MaxFrames {
   619						v.err = ErrStackOverflow
   620						return
   621					}
   622	
   623					// update call frame
   624					v.curFrame.ip = v.ip // store current ip before call
   625					v.curFrame = &(v.frames[v.framesIndex])
   626					v.curFrame.fn = callee
   627					v.curFrame.freeVars = callee.Free
   628					v.curFrame.basePointer = v.sp - numArgs
   629					v.curInsts = callee.Instructions
   630					v.ip = -1
   631					v.framesIndex++
   632					v.sp = v.sp - numArgs + callee.NumLocals
   633				} else {
   634					var args []Object
   635					args = append(args, v.stack[v.sp-numArgs:v.sp]...)
   636					ret, e := value.Call(args...)
   637					v.sp -= numArgs + 1
   638	
   639					// runtime error
   640					if e != nil {
   641						if e == ErrWrongNumArguments {
   642							v.err = fmt.Errorf(
   643								"wrong number of arguments in call to '%s'",
   644								value.TypeName())
   645							return
   646						}
   647						if e, ok := e.(ErrInvalidArgumentType); ok {
   648							v.err = fmt.Errorf(
   649								"invalid type for argument '%s' in call to '%s': "+
   650									"expected %s, found %s",
   651								e.Name, value.TypeName(), e.Expected, e.Found)
   652							return
   653						}
   654						v.err = e
   655						return
   656					}
   657	
   658					// nil return -> undefined
   659					if ret == nil {
   660						ret = UndefinedValue
   661					}
   662					v.allocs--
   663					if v.allocs == 0 {
   664						v.err = ErrObjectAllocLimit
   665						return
   666					}
   667					v.stack[v.sp] = ret
   668					v.sp++
   669				}

package tengo

// SymbolScope represents a symbol scope.
type SymbolScope string

// List of symbol scopes
const (
	ScopeGlobal  SymbolScope = "GLOBAL"
	ScopeLocal   SymbolScope = "LOCAL"
	ScopeBuiltin SymbolScope = "BUILTIN"
	ScopeFree    SymbolScope = "FREE"
)

// Symbol represents a symbol in the symbol table.
type Symbol struct {
	Name          string
	Scope         SymbolScope
	Index         int
	LocalAssigned bool // if the local symbol is assigned at least once
}

// SymbolTable represents a symbol table.
type SymbolTable struct {
	parent         *SymbolTable
	block          bool
	store          map[string]*Symbol
	numDefinition  int
	maxDefinition  int
	freeSymbols    []*Symbol
	builtinSymbols []*Symbol
}

// NewSymbolTable creates a SymbolTable.
func NewSymbolTable() *SymbolTable {
	return &SymbolTable{
		store: make(map[string]*Symbol),
	}
}

// Define adds a new symbol in the current scope.
func (t *SymbolTable) Define(name string) *Symbol {
	symbol := &Symbol{Name: name, Index: t.nextIndex()}
	t.numDefinition++

	if t.Parent(true) == nil {
		symbol.Scope = ScopeGlobal

		// if symbol is defined in a block of global scope, symbol index must
		// be tracked at the root-level table instead.
		if p := t.parent; p != nil {
			for p.parent != nil {
				p = p.parent
			}
			t.numDefinition--
			p.numDefinition++
		}

	} else {
		symbol.Scope = ScopeLocal
	}
	t.store[name] = symbol
	t.updateMaxDefs(symbol.Index + 1)
	return symbol
}

// DefineBuiltin adds a symbol for builtin function.
func (t *SymbolTable) DefineBuiltin(index int, name string) *Symbol {
	if t.parent != nil {
		return t.parent.DefineBuiltin(index, name)
	}

	symbol := &Symbol{
		Name:  name,
		Index: index,
		Scope: ScopeBuiltin,
	}
	t.store[name] = symbol
	t.builtinSymbols = append(t.builtinSymbols, symbol)
	return symbol
}

// Resolve resolves a symbol with a given name.
func (t *SymbolTable) Resolve(
	name string,
	recur bool,
) (*Symbol, int, bool) {
	symbol, ok := t.store[name]
	if ok {
		// symbol can be used if
		if symbol.Scope != ScopeLocal || // it's not of local scope, OR,
			symbol.LocalAssigned || // it's assigned at least once, OR,
			recur { // it's defined in higher level
			return symbol, 0, true
		}
	}

	if t.parent == nil {
		return nil, 0, false
	}

	symbol, depth, ok := t.parent.Resolve(name, true)
	if !ok {
		return nil, 0, false
	}
	depth++

	// if symbol is defined in parent table and if it's not global/builtin
	// then it's free variable.
	if !t.block && depth > 0 &&
		symbol.Scope != ScopeGlobal &&
		symbol.Scope != ScopeBuiltin {
		return t.defineFree(symbol), depth, true
	}
	return symbol, depth, true
}

// Fork creates a new symbol table for a new scope.
func (t *SymbolTable) Fork(block bool) *SymbolTable {
	return &SymbolTable{
		store:  make(map[string]*Symbol),
		parent: t,
		block:  block,
	}
}

// Parent returns the outer scope of the current symbol table.
func (t *SymbolTable) Parent(skipBlock bool) *SymbolTable {
	if skipBlock && t.block {
		return t.parent.Parent(skipBlock)
	}
	return t.parent
}

// MaxSymbols returns the total number of symbols defined in the scope.
func (t *SymbolTable) MaxSymbols() int {
	return t.maxDefinition
}

// FreeSymbols returns free symbols for the scope.
func (t *SymbolTable) FreeSymbols() []*Symbol {
	return t.freeSymbols
}

// BuiltinSymbols returns builtin symbols for the scope.
func (t *SymbolTable) BuiltinSymbols() []*Symbol {
	if t.parent != nil {
		return t.parent.BuiltinSymbols()
	}
	return t.builtinSymbols
}

// Names returns the name of all the symbols.
func (t *SymbolTable) Names() []string {
	var names []string
	for name := range t.store {
		names = append(names, name)
	}
	return names
}

func (t *SymbolTable) nextIndex() int {
	if t.block {
		return t.parent.nextIndex() + t.numDefinition
	}
	return t.numDefinition
}

func (t *SymbolTable) updateMaxDefs(numDefs int) {
	if numDefs > t.maxDefinition {
		t.maxDefinition = numDefs
	}
	if t.block {
		t.parent.updateMaxDefs(numDefs)
	}
}

func (t *SymbolTable) defineFree(original *Symbol) *Symbol {
	// TODO: should we check duplicates?
	t.freeSymbols = append(t.freeSymbols, original)
	symbol := &Symbol{
		Name:  original.Name,
		Index: len(t.freeSymbols) - 1,
		Scope: ScopeFree,
	}
	t.store[original.Name] = symbol
	return symbol
}

package tengo_test

import (
	"errors"

const testOut = "out"

type IARR []interface{}
type IMAP map[string]interface{}
type MAP = map[string]interface{}
type ARR = []interface{}

type testopts struct {
	modules     *tengo.ModuleMap
	symbols     map[string]tengo.Object
	maxAllocs   int64
	skip2ndPass bool
}

func Opts() *testopts {
	return &testopts{
		modules:     tengo.NewModuleMap(),
		symbols:     make(map[string]tengo.Object),
		maxAllocs:   -1,
		skip2ndPass: false,
	}
}

func (o *testopts) copy() *testopts {
	c := &testopts{
		modules:     o.modules.Copy(),
		symbols:     make(map[string]tengo.Object),
		maxAllocs:   o.maxAllocs,
		skip2ndPass: o.skip2ndPass,
	}
	for k, v := range o.symbols {
		c.symbols[k] = v
	}
	return c
}

func (o *testopts) Stdlib() *testopts {
	o.modules.AddMap(stdlib.GetModuleMap(stdlib.AllModuleNames()...))
	return o
}

func TestIdentListString(t *testing.T) {
	identListVar := &parser.IdentList{
		List: []*parser.Ident{
			{Name: "a"},
			{Name: "b"},
			{Name: "c"},
		},
		VarArgs: true,
	}

	expectedVar := "(a, b, ...c)"
	if str := identListVar.String(); str != expectedVar {
		t.Fatalf("expected string of %#v to be %s, got %s",
			identListVar, expectedVar, str)

	identList := &parser.IdentList{
		List: []*parser.Ident{
			{Name: "a"},
			{Name: "b"},
			{Name: "c"},
		},
		VarArgs: false,
	}

	expected := "(a, b, c)"
	if str := identList.String(); str != expected {
		t.Fatalf("expected string of %#v to be %s, got %s",
			identList, expected, str)
	}

  1340	func resolveAssignLHS(
  1341		expr parser.Expr,
  1342	) (name string, selectors []parser.Expr) {
  1343		switch term := expr.(type) {
  1344		case *parser.SelectorExpr:
  1345			name, selectors = resolveAssignLHS(term.Expr)
  1346			selectors = append(selectors, term.Sel)
  1347			return
  1348		case *parser.IndexExpr:
  1349			name, selectors = resolveAssignLHS(term.Expr)
  1350			selectors = append(selectors, term.Index)
  1351		case *parser.Ident:
  1352			name = term.Name
  1353		}
  1354		return
  1355	}
  1356	
  1357	func iterateInstructions(
  1358		b []byte,
  1359		fn func(pos int, opcode parser.Opcode, operands []int) bool,
  1360	) {
  1361		for i := 0; i < len(b); i++ {
  1362			numOperands := parser.OpcodeOperands[b[i]]
  1363			operands, read := parser.ReadOperands(numOperands, b[i+1:])
  1364			if !fn(i, b[i], operands) {
  1365				break
  1366			}
  1367			i += read
  1368		}
  1369	}
  1370	
  1371	func tracec(c *Compiler, msg string) *Compiler {
  1372		c.printTrace(msg, "{")
  1373		c.indent++
  1374		return c
  1375	}
  1376	
  1377	func untracec(c *Compiler) {
  1378		c.indent--
  1379		c.printTrace("}")
  1380	}

   222	// IndexGet returns an element at a given index.
   223	func (o *Array) IndexGet(index Object) (res Object, err error) {
   224		intIdx, ok := index.(*Int)
   225		if !ok {
   226			err = ErrInvalidIndexType
   227			return
   228		}
   229		idxVal := int(intIdx.Value)
   230		if idxVal < 0 || idxVal >= len(o.Value) {
   231			res = UndefinedValue
   232			return
   233		}
   234		res = o.Value[idxVal]
   235		return
   236	}
   237	
   238	// IndexSet sets an element at a given index.
   239	func (o *Array) IndexSet(index, value Object) (err error) {
   240		intIdx, ok := ToInt(index)
   241		if !ok {
   242			err = ErrInvalidIndexType
   243			return
   244		}
   245		if intIdx < 0 || intIdx >= len(o.Value) {
   246			err = ErrIndexOutOfBounds
   247			return
   248		}
   249		o.Value[intIdx] = value
   250		return nil
   251	}

  1268	// Iterate creates a map iterator.
  1269	func (o *Map) Iterate() Iterator {

  1273		}

  1275			v: o.Value,
  1276			k: keys,
  1277			l: len(keys),
  1278		}
  1279	}

=== traceCompileRun ===
  3801	func traceCompileRun(
  3802		file *parser.File,
  3803		symbols map[string]tengo.Object,
  3804		modules *tengo.ModuleMap,
  3805		maxAllocs int64,
  3806	) (res map[string]tengo.Object, trace []string, err error) {
  3807		var v *tengo.VM
  3808	
  3809		defer func() {
  3810			if e := recover(); e != nil {
  3811				err = fmt.Errorf("panic: %v", e)
  3812	
  3813				// stack trace
  3814				var stackTrace []string
  3815				for i := 2; ; i += 1 {
  3816					_, file, line, ok := _runtime.Caller(i)
  3817					if !ok {
  3818						break
  3819					}
  3820					stackTrace = append(stackTrace,
  3821						fmt.Sprintf("  %s:%d", file, line))
  3822				}
  3823	
  3824				trace = append(trace,
  3825					fmt.Sprintf("[Error Trace]\n\n  %s\n",
  3826						strings.Join(stackTrace, "\n  ")))
  3827			}
  3828		}()
  3829	
  3830		globals := make([]tengo.Object, tengo.GlobalsSize)
  3831	
  3832		symTable := tengo.NewSymbolTable()
  3833		for name, value := range symbols {
  3834			sym := symTable.Define(name)
  3835	
  3836			// should not store pointer to 'value' variable
  3837			// which is re-used in each iteration.
  3838			valueCopy := value
  3839			globals[sym.Index] = valueCopy
  3840		}
  3841		for idx, fn := range tengo.GetAllBuiltinFunctions() {
  3842			symTable.DefineBuiltin(idx, fn.Name)
  3843		}
  3844	
  3845		tr := &vmTracer{}
  3846		c := tengo.NewCompiler(file.InputFile, symTable, nil, modules, tr)
  3847		err = c.Compile(file)
  3848		trace = append(trace,
  3849			fmt.Sprintf("\n[Compiler Trace]\n\n%s",
  3850				strings.Join(tr.Out, "")))
  3851		if err != nil {
  3852			return
  3853		}
  3854	
  3855		bytecode := c.Bytecode()
  3856		bytecode.RemoveDuplicates()
  3857		trace = append(trace, fmt.Sprintf("\n[Compiled Constants]\n\n%s",
  3858			strings.Join(bytecode.FormatConstants(), "\n")))
  3859		trace = append(trace, fmt.Sprintf("\n[Compiled Instructions]\n\n%s\n",
  3860			strings.Join(bytecode.FormatInstructions(), "\n")))
  3861	
  3862		v = tengo.NewVM(bytecode, globals, maxAllocs)
  3863	
  3864		err = v.Run()
  3865		{
  3866			res = make(map[string]tengo.Object)
  3867			for name := range symbols {
  3868				sym, depth, ok := symTable.Resolve(name, false)
  3869				if !ok || depth != 0 {
  3870					err = fmt.Errorf("symbol not found: %s", name)
  3871					return
  3872				}
  3873	
  3874				res[name] = globals[sym.Index]
  3875			}
  3876			trace = append(trace, fmt.Sprintf("\n[Globals]\n\n%s",
  3877				strings.Join(formatGlobals(globals), "\n")))
  3878		}
  3879		if err == nil && !v.IsStackEmpty() {
  3880			err = errors.New("non empty stack after execution")
  3881		}
  3882	
  3883		return
  3884	}
  3885	
  3886	func formatGlobals(globals []tengo.Object) (formatted []string) {
  3887		for idx, global := range globals {
  3888			if global == nil {
  3889				return
  3890			}
  3891			formatted = append(formatted, fmt.Sprintf("[% 3d] %s (%s|%p)",
  3892				idx, global.String(), reflect.TypeOf(global).Elem().Name(), global))
  3893		}
  3894		return
  3895	}

    28	
    29	func (e Error) Error() string {
    30		if e.Pos.Filename != "" || e.Pos.IsValid() {
    31			return fmt.Sprintf("Parse Error: %s\n\tat %s", e.Msg, e.Pos)
    32		}
    33		return fmt.Sprintf("Parse Error: %s", e.Msg)
    34	}
