    38	
    39	// Add adds a new parser error to the collection.
    40	func (p *ErrorList) Add(pos SourceFilePos, msg string) {
    41		*p = append(*p, &Error{pos, msg})
    42	}
    43	
    44	// Len returns the number of elements in the collection.
    45	func (p ErrorList) Len() int {
    46		return len(p)
    47	}
    48	
    49	func (p ErrorList) Swap(i, j int) {
    50		p[i], p[j] = p[j], p[i]
    51	}
    52	
    53	func (p ErrorList) Less(i, j int) bool {
    54		e := &p[i].Pos
    55		f := &p[j].Pos
    56	
    57		if e.Filename != f.Filename {
    58			return e.Filename < f.Filename
    59		}
    60		if e.Line != f.Line {
    61			return e.Line < f.Line
    62		}
    63		if e.Column != f.Column {
    64			return e.Column < f.Column
    65		}
    66		return p[i].Msg < p[j].Msg
    67	}
    68	
    69	// Sort sorts the collection.
    70	func (p ErrorList) Sort() {
    71		sort.Sort(p)
    72	}
    73	
    74	func (p ErrorList) Error() string {
    75		switch len(p) {
    76		case 0:
    77			return "no errors"
    78		case 1:
    79			return p[0].Error()
    80		}
    81		return fmt.Sprintf("%s (and %d more errors)", p[0], len(p)-1)
    82	}
    83	
    84	// Err returns an error.
    85	func (p ErrorList) Err() error {
    86		if len(p) == 0 {
    87			return nil
    88		}
    89		return p
    90	}
    91	
    92	// Parser parses the Tengo source files. It's based on Go's parser
    93	// implementation.
    94	type Parser struct {
    95		file      *SourceFile
    96		errors    ErrorList
    97		scanner   *Scanner
    98		pos       Pos
    99		token     token.Token
   100		tokenLit  string
   101		exprLevel int // < 0: in control clause, >= 0: in expression
   102		syncPos   Pos // last sync position
   103		syncCount int // number of advance calls without progress
   104		trace     bool
   105		indent    int
   106		traceOut  io.Writer
   107	}
   108	
   109	// NewParser creates a Parser.
   110	func NewParser(file *SourceFile, src []byte, trace io.Writer) *Parser {
   111		p := &Parser{
   112			file:     file,
   113			trace:    trace != nil,
   114			traceOut: trace,
   115		}
   116		p.scanner = NewScanner(p.file, src,
   117			func(pos SourceFilePos, msg string) {
   118				p.errors.Add(pos, msg)
   119			}, 0)
   120		p.next()
   121		return p
   122	}
   123	
   124	// ParseFile parses the source and returns an AST file unit.
   125	func (p *Parser) ParseFile() (file *File, err error) {
   126		defer func() {
   127			if e := recover(); e != nil {
   128				if _, ok := e.(bailout); !ok {
   129					panic(e)
   130				}
   131			}
   132	
   133			p.errors.Sort()
   134			err = p.errors.Err()
   135		}()
   136	
   137		if p.trace {
   138			defer untracep(tracep(p, "File"))
   139		}
   140	
   141		if p.errors.Len() > 0 {
   142			return nil, p.errors.Err()
   143		}
   144	
   145		stmts := p.parseStmtList()
   146		p.expect(token.EOF)
   147		if p.errors.Len() > 0 {
   148			return nil, p.errors.Err()
   149		}
   150	
   151		file = &File{
   152			InputFile: p.file,
   153			Stmts:     stmts,
   154		}
   155		return
   156	}
   157	
   158	func (p *Parser) parseExpr() Expr {
   159		if p.trace {
   160			defer untracep(tracep(p, "Expression"))
   161		}
   162	
   163		expr := p.parseBinaryExpr(token.LowestPrec + 1)
   164	
   165		// ternary conditional expression
   166		if p.token == token.Question {
   167			return p.parseCondExpr(expr)
   168		}
   169		return expr
   170	}
   171	
   172	func (p *Parser) parseBinaryExpr(prec1 int) Expr {
   173		if p.trace {
   174			defer untracep(tracep(p, "BinaryExpression"))
   175		}
   176	
   177		x := p.parseUnaryExpr()
   178	
   179		for {
   180			op, prec := p.token, p.token.Precedence()
   181			if prec < prec1 {
   182				return x
   183			}
   184	
   185			pos := p.expect(op)
   186	
   187			y := p.parseBinaryExpr(prec + 1)
   188	
   189			x = &BinaryExpr{
   190				LHS:      x,
   191				RHS:      y,
   192				Token:    op,
   193				TokenPos: pos,
   194			}
   195		}
   196	}
   197	
   198	func (p *Parser) parseCondExpr(cond Expr) Expr {
   199		questionPos := p.expect(token.Question)
   200		trueExpr := p.parseExpr()
   201		colonPos := p.expect(token.Colon)
   202		falseExpr := p.parseExpr()
   203	
   204		return &CondExpr{
   205			Cond:        cond,
   206			True:        trueExpr,
   207			False:       falseExpr,
   208			QuestionPos: questionPos,
   209			ColonPos:    colonPos,
   210		}
   211	}
   212	
   213	func (p *Parser) parseUnaryExpr() Expr {
   214		if p.trace {
   215			defer untracep(tracep(p, "UnaryExpression"))
   216		}
   217	
   218		switch p.token {
   219		case token.Add, token.Sub, token.Not, token.Xor:
   220			pos, op := p.pos, p.token
   221			p.next()
   222			x := p.parseUnaryExpr()
   223			return &UnaryExpr{
   224				Token:    op,
   225				TokenPos: pos,
   226				Expr:     x,
   227			}
   228		}
   229		return p.parsePrimaryExpr()
   230	}

    41	// NewScanner creates a Scanner.

    43		file *SourceFile,
    44		src []byte,
    45		errorHandler ScannerErrorHandler,
    46		mode ScanMode,
    47	) *Scanner {
    48		if file.Size != len(src) {

    50				file.Size, len(src)))
    51		}
    52

    55			src:          src,

    57			ch:           ' ',
    58			mode:         mode,
    59		}

    62		if s.ch == bom {
    63			s.next() // ignore BOM at file beginning
    64		}
    65	
    66		return s
    67	}

    73	
    74	// Scan returns a token, token literal and its position.
    75	func (s *Scanner) Scan() (
    76		tok token.Token,
    77		literal string,
    78		pos Pos,
    79	) {
    80		s.skipWhitespace()
    81	
    82		pos = s.file.FileSetPos(s.offset)
    83	
    84		insertSemi := false
    85	
    86		// determine token value
    87		switch ch := s.ch; {
    88		case isLetter(ch):
    89			literal = s.scanIdentifier()
    90			tok = token.Lookup(literal)
    91			switch tok {
    92			case token.Ident, token.Break, token.Continue, token.Return,
    93				token.Export, token.True, token.False, token.Undefined:
    94				insertSemi = true
    95			}
    96		case ('0' <= ch && ch <= '9') || (ch == '.' && '0' <= s.peek() && s.peek() <= '9'):
    97			insertSemi = true
    98			tok, literal = s.scanNumber()
    99		default:
   100			s.next() // always make progress
   101	
   102			switch ch {
   103			case -1: // EOF
   104				if s.insertSemi {
   105					s.insertSemi = false // EOF consumed
   106					return token.Semicolon, "\n", pos
   107				}
   108				tok = token.EOF
   109			case '\n':
   110				// we only reach here if s.insertSemi was set in the first place
   111				s.insertSemi = false // newline consumed
   112				return token.Semicolon, "\n", pos
   113			case '"':
   114				insertSemi = true
   115				tok = token.String
   116				literal = s.scanString()
   117			case '\'':
   118				insertSemi = true
   119				tok = token.Char
   120				literal = s.scanRune()
   121			case '`':
   122				insertSemi = true
   123				tok = token.String
   124				literal = s.scanRawString()
   125			case ':':
   126				tok = s.switch2(token.Colon, token.Define)
   127			case '.':
   128				tok = token.Period
   129				if s.ch == '.' && s.peek() == '.' {
   130					s.next()
   131					s.next() // consume last '.'
   132					tok = token.Ellipsis
   133				}
   134			case ',':
   135				tok = token.Comma
   136			case '?':
   137				tok = token.Question
   138			case ';':
   139				tok = token.Semicolon
   140				literal = ";"

  1099	func (p *Parser) expectSemi() {
  1100		switch p.token {
  1101		case token.RParen, token.RBrace:
  1102			// semicolon is optional before a closing ')' or '}'
  1103		case token.Comma:
  1104			// permit a ',' instead of a ';' but complain

  1115	func (p *Parser) advance(to map[token.Token]bool) {
  1116		for ; p.token != token.EOF; p.next() {
  1117			if to[p.token] {
  1118				if p.pos == p.syncPos && p.syncCount < 10 {
  1119					p.syncCount++
  1120					return
  1121				}
  1122				if p.pos > p.syncPos {
  1123					p.syncPos = p.pos
  1124					p.syncCount = 0
  1125					return
  1126				}
  1127			}
  1128		}
  1129	}
  1130	
  1131	func (p *Parser) error(pos Pos, msg string) {
  1132		filePos := p.file.Position(pos)
  1133	
  1134		n := len(p.errors)
  1135		if n > 0 && p.errors[n-1].Pos.Line == filePos.Line {
  1136			// discard errors reported on the same line
  1137			return
  1138		}
  1139		if n > 10 {
  1140			// too many errors; terminate early
  1141			panic(bailout{})
  1142		}
  1143		p.errors.Add(filePos, msg)
  1144	}
  1145	
  1146	func (p *Parser) errorExpected(pos Pos, msg string) {
  1147		msg = "expected " + msg
  1148		if pos == p.pos {
  1149			// error happened at the current position: provide more specific
  1150			switch {
  1151			case p.token == token.Semicolon && p.tokenLit == "\n":
  1152				msg += ", found newline"
  1153			case p.token.IsLiteral():
  1154				msg += ", found " + p.tokenLit
  1155			default:
  1156				msg += ", found '" + p.token.String() + "'"
  1157			}
  1158		}
  1159		p.error(pos, msg)
  1160	}

package parser

import (
	"fmt"
	"sort"
)

// SourceFilePos represents a position information in the file.
type SourceFilePos struct {
	Filename string // filename, if any
	Offset   int    // offset, starting at 0
	Line     int    // line number, starting at 1
	Column   int    // column number, starting at 1 (byte count)
}

// IsValid returns true if the position is valid.
func (p SourceFilePos) IsValid() bool {
	return p.Line > 0
}

// String returns a string in one of several forms:
//
//	file:line:column    valid position with file name
//	file:line           valid position with file name but no column (column == 0)
//	line:column         valid position without file name
//	line                valid position without file name and no column (column == 0)
//	file                invalid position with file name
//	-                   invalid position without file name
func (p SourceFilePos) String() string {
	s := p.Filename
	if p.IsValid() {
		if s != "" {
			s += ":"
		}
		s += fmt.Sprintf("%d", p.Line)
		if p.Column != 0 {
			s += fmt.Sprintf(":%d", p.Column)
		}
	}
	if s == "" {
		s = "-"
	}
	return s
}

// SourceFileSet represents a set of source files.
type SourceFileSet struct {
	Base     int           // base offset for the next file
	Files    []*SourceFile // list of files in the order added to the set
	LastFile *SourceFile   // cache of last file looked up
}

// NewFileSet creates a new file set.
func NewFileSet() *SourceFileSet {
	return &SourceFileSet{
		Base: 1, // 0 == NoPos
	}
}

// Size of all files in this set in bytes.
func (s *SourceFileSet) Size() (size int64) {
	for _, f := range s.Files {
		size += int64(f.Size)
	}
	return size
}

// AddFile adds a new file in the file set.
func (s *SourceFileSet) AddFile(filename string, base, size int) *SourceFile {
	if base < 0 {
		base = s.Base
	}
	if base < s.Base || size < 0 {
		panic("illegal base or size")
	}
	f := &SourceFile{
		set:   s,
		Name:  filename,
		Base:  base,
		Size:  size,
		Lines: []int{0},
	}
	base += size + 1 // +1 because EOF also has a position
	if base < 0 {
		panic("offset overflow (> 2G of source code in file set)")
	}

	// add the file to the file set
	s.Base = base
	s.Files = append(s.Files, f)
	s.LastFile = f
	return f
}

// File returns the file that contains the position p. If no such file is
// found (for instance for p == NoPos), the result is nil.
func (s *SourceFileSet) File(p Pos) (f *SourceFile) {
	if p != NoPos {
		f = s.file(p)
	}
	return
}

// Position converts a SourcePos p in the fileset into a SourceFilePos value.
func (s *SourceFileSet) Position(p Pos) (pos SourceFilePos) {
	if p != NoPos {
		if f := s.file(p); f != nil {
			return f.position(p)
		}
	}
	return
}

func (s *SourceFileSet) file(p Pos) *SourceFile {
	// common case: p is in last file
	f := s.LastFile
	if f != nil && f.Base <= int(p) && int(p) <= f.Base+f.Size {
		return f
	}

	// p is not in last file - search all files
	if i := searchFiles(s.Files, int(p)); i >= 0 {
		f := s.Files[i]

		// f.base <= int(p) by definition of searchFiles
		if int(p) <= f.Base+f.Size {
			s.LastFile = f // race is ok - s.last is only a cache
			return f
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func searchFiles(a []*SourceFile, x int) int {
	return sort.Search(len(a), func(i int) bool { return a[i].Base > x }) - 1
}

// SourceFile represents a source file.
type SourceFile struct {
	// SourceFile set for the file
	set *SourceFileSet
	// SourceFile name as provided to AddFile
	Name string
	// SourcePos value range for this file is [base...base+size]
	Base int
	// SourceFile size as provided to AddFile
	Size int
	// Lines contains the offset of the first character for each line
	// (the first entry is always 0)
	Lines []int
}

// Set returns SourceFileSet.
func (f *SourceFile) Set() *SourceFileSet {
	return f.set
}

// LineCount returns the current number of lines.
func (f *SourceFile) LineCount() int {
	return len(f.Lines)
}

// AddLine adds a new line.
func (f *SourceFile) AddLine(offset int) {
	i := len(f.Lines)
	if (i == 0 || f.Lines[i-1] < offset) && offset < f.Size {
		f.Lines = append(f.Lines, offset)
	}
}

// LineStart returns the position of the first character in the line.
func (f *SourceFile) LineStart(line int) Pos {
	if line < 1 {
		panic("illegal line number (line numbering starts at 1)")
	}
	if line > len(f.Lines) {
		panic("illegal line number")
	}
	return Pos(f.Base + f.Lines[line-1])
}

// FileSetPos returns the position in the file set.
func (f *SourceFile) FileSetPos(offset int) Pos {
	if offset > f.Size {
		panic("illegal file offset")
	}
	return Pos(f.Base + offset)
}

// Offset translates the file set position into the file offset.
func (f *SourceFile) Offset(p Pos) int {
	if int(p) < f.Base || int(p) > f.Base+f.Size {
		panic("illegal SourcePos value")
	}
	return int(p) - f.Base
}

// Position translates the file set position into the file position.
func (f *SourceFile) Position(p Pos) (pos SourceFilePos) {
	if p != NoPos {
		if int(p) < f.Base || int(p) > f.Base+f.Size {
			panic("illegal SourcePos value")
		}
		pos = f.position(p)
	}
	return
}

func (f *SourceFile) position(p Pos) (pos SourceFilePos) {
	offset := int(p) - f.Base
	pos.Offset = offset
	pos.Filename, pos.Line, pos.Column = f.unpack(offset)
	return
}

func (f *SourceFile) unpack(offset int) (filename string, line, column int) {
	filename = f.Name
	if i := searchInts(f.Lines, offset); i >= 0 {
		line, column = i+1, offset-f.Lines[i]+1
	}
	return
}

func searchInts(a []int, x int) int {
	// This function body is a manually inlined version of:
	//   return sort.Search(len(a), func(i int) bool { return a[i] > x }) - 1
	i, j := 0, len(a)
	for i < j {
		h := i + (j-i)/2 // avoid overflow when computing h
		// i ≤ h < j
		if a[h] <= x {
			i = h + 1
		} else {
			j = h
		}
	}
	return i - 1
}
package parser

import (
	"strings"
)

// File represents a file unit.
type File struct {
	InputFile *SourceFile
	Stmts     []Stmt
}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (n *File) Pos() Pos {
	return Pos(n.InputFile.Base)
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (n *File) End() Pos {
	return Pos(n.InputFile.Base + n.InputFile.Size)
}

func (n *File) String() string {
	var stmts []string
	for _, e := range n.Stmts {
		stmts = append(stmts, e.String())
	}
	return strings.Join(stmts, "; ")
}

package tengo

import (
	"strconv"
	"sync"
	"unicode/utf8"
)

// Strings for use with fmtbuf.WriteString. This is less overhead than using
// fmtbuf.Write with byte arrays.
const (
	commaSpaceString  = ", "
	nilParenString    = "(nil)"
	percentBangString = "%!"
	missingString     = "(MISSING)"
	badIndexString    = "(BADINDEX)"
	extraString       = "%!(EXTRA "
	badWidthString    = "%!(BADWIDTH)"
	badPrecString     = "%!(BADPREC)"
	noVerbString      = "%!(NOVERB)"
)

const (
	ldigits = "0123456789abcdefx"
	udigits = "0123456789ABCDEFX"
)

const (
	signed   = true
	unsigned = false
)

// flags placed in a separate struct for easy clearing.
type fmtFlags struct {
	widPresent  bool
	precPresent bool
	minus       bool
	plus        bool
	sharp       bool
	space       bool
	zero        bool

	// For the formats %+v %#v, we set the plusV/sharpV flags
	// and clear the plus/sharp flags since %+v and %#v are in effect
	// different, flagless formats set at the top level.
	plusV  bool
	sharpV bool

	// error-related flags.
	inDetail    bool
	needNewline bool
	needColon   bool
}

// A formatter is the raw formatter used by Printf etc.
// It prints into a fmtbuf that must be set up separately.
type formatter struct {
	buf *fmtbuf

	fmtFlags

	wid  int // width
	prec int // precision

	// intbuf is large enough to store %b of an int64 with a sign and
	// avoids padding at the end of the struct on 32 bit architectures.
	intbuf [68]byte
}

func (f *formatter) clearFlags() {
	f.fmtFlags = fmtFlags{}
}

func (f *formatter) init(buf *fmtbuf) {
	f.buf = buf
	f.clearFlags()
}

// writePadding generates n bytes of padding.
func (f *formatter) writePadding(n int) {

  2056	func equalExpr(t *testing.T, expected, actual Expr) {
  2057		if expected == nil || reflect.ValueOf(expected).IsNil() {
  2058			require.Nil(t, actual, "expected nil, but got not nil")
  2059			return
  2060		}
  2061		require.NotNil(t, actual, "expected not nil, but got nil")

  2063

  2065		case *Ident:
  2066			require.Equal(t, expected.Name,
  2067				actual.(*Ident).Name)
  2068			require.Equal(t, int(expected.NamePos),
  2069				int(actual.(*Ident).NamePos))

  2074				int(actual.(*IntLit).ValuePos))

import (

func run(src string) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	s.SetImports(nil)

		fmt.Printf("ERR: %v\n", err)

	run(`{x: a, y} := {x: 10, y: 20}`)
	run(`[a, ...rest] := [1, 2, 3]`)
	run(`[a = 5] := []`)
}

STEP 54 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cat > /app/parser/pattern.go <<'EOF'
package parser

import (
	"strings"
)

// ArrayPattern represents an array destructuring pattern.
type ArrayPattern struct {
	LBrack   Pos
	Elements []*ArrayPatternElement
	RBrack   Pos
}

func (e *ArrayPattern) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *ArrayPattern) Pos() Pos {
	return e.LBrack
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *ArrayPattern) End() Pos {
	return e.RBrack + 1
}

func (e *ArrayPattern) String() string {
	var elements []string
	for _, elt := range e.Elements {
		elements = append(elements, elt.String())
	}
	return "[" + strings.Join(elements, ", ") + "]"
}

// ArrayPatternElement represents an element of an array pattern.
type ArrayPatternElement struct {
	Value       Expr
	Rest        bool
	EllipsisPos Pos
	Default     Expr
}

func (e *ArrayPatternElement) String() string {
	var s string
	if e.Rest {
		s = "..."
	}
	s += e.Value.String()
	if e.Default != nil {
		s += " = " + e.Default.String()
	}
	return s
}

// MapPattern represents a map destructuring pattern.
type MapPattern struct {
	LBrace Pos
	Fields []*MapPatternField
	RBrace Pos
}

func (e *MapPattern) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *MapPattern) Pos() Pos {
	return e.LBrace
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *MapPattern) End() Pos {
	return e.RBrace + 1
}

func (e *MapPattern) String() string {
	var fields []string
	for _, f := range e.Fields {
		fields = append(fields, f.String())
	}
	return "{" + strings.Join(fields, ", ") + "}"
}

// MapPatternField represents a field of a map pattern.
type MapPatternField struct {
	Key      string
	KeyPos   Pos
	ColonPos Pos
	Value    Expr
	Default  Expr
}

func (e *MapPatternField) String() string {
	var s string
	if e.ColonPos.IsValid() {
		s = e.Key + ": " + e.Value.String()
	} else {
		s = e.Value.String()
	}
	if e.Default != nil {
		s += " = " + e.Default.String()
	}
	return s
}
EOF
echo done
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
done

END STEP

p='parser/parser.go'

# Replace parseIdentList function with pattern-aware version and add pattern parsers after it.
old = '''func (p *Parser) parseIdentList() *IdentList {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "IdentList"))
	}

	var params []*Ident
	lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
	isVarArgs := false
	if p.token != token.RParen {
		if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
			isVarArgs = true
			p.next()
		}

		params = append(params, p.parseIdent())
		for !isVarArgs && p.token == token.Comma {
			p.next()
			if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
				isVarArgs = true
				p.next()
			}
			params = append(params, p.parseIdent())
		}
	}

	rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
	return &IdentList{
		LParen:  lparen,
		RParen:  rparen,
		VarArgs: isVarArgs,
		List:    params,
	}
}
'''

new = '''func (p *Parser) parseIdentList() *IdentList {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "IdentList"))
	}

	var params []Expr
	lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
	isVarArgs := false
	if p.token != token.RParen {
		if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
			isVarArgs = true
			p.next()
		}

		params = append(params, p.parsePatternParam())
		for !isVarArgs && p.token == token.Comma {
			p.next()
			if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
				isVarArgs = true
				p.next()
			}
			params = append(params, p.parsePatternParam())
		}
	}

	rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
	return &IdentList{
		LParen:  lparen,
		RParen:  rparen,
		VarArgs: isVarArgs,
		List:    params,
	}
}

// parsePatternParam parses a function parameter, which can be an identifier,
// an array pattern, or a map pattern.
func (p *Parser) parsePatternParam() Expr {
	switch p.token {
	case token.LBrack:
		return p.parseArrayPattern()
	case token.LBrace:
		return p.parseMapPattern()
	default:
		return p.parseIdent()
	}
}

// parsePattern parses a destructuring pattern.
func (p *Parser) parsePattern() Expr {
	switch p.token {
	case token.LBrack:
		return p.parseArrayPattern()
	case token.LBrace:
		return p.parseMapPattern()
	default:
		return p.parseIdent()
	}
}

// parseArrayPattern parses an array destructuring pattern.
func (p *Parser) parseArrayPattern() Expr {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "ArrayPattern"))
	}

	lbrack := p.expect(token.LBrack)
	var elements []*ArrayPatternElement
	for p.token != token.RBrack && p.token != token.EOF {
		var (
			rest        bool
			ellipsisPos Pos
		)
		if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
			rest = true
			ellipsisPos = p.pos
			p.next()
		}

		value := p.parsePattern()
		var def Expr
		if p.token == token.Assign {
			if rest {
				p.error(p.pos, "default value not allowed in rest element")
			}
			p.next()
			def = p.parseExpr()
		}

		elements = append(elements, &ArrayPatternElement{
			Value:       value,
			Rest:        rest,
			EllipsisPos: ellipsisPos,
			Default:     def,
		})

		if rest && p.token == token.Comma {
			p.error(p.pos, "rest element must be last")
		}
		if !p.expectComma(token.RBrack, "array pattern element") {
			break
		}
	}
	rbrack := p.expect(token.RBrack)
	return &ArrayPattern{
		LBrack:   lbrack,
		RBrack:   rbrack,
		Elements: elements,
	}
}

// parseMapPattern parses a map destructuring pattern.
func (p *Parser) parseMapPattern() Expr {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "MapPattern"))
	}

	lbrace := p.expect(token.LBrace)
	var fields []*MapPatternField
	for p.token != token.RBrace && p.token != token.EOF {
		pos := p.pos
		name := "_"
		if p.token == token.Ident {
			name = p.tokenLit
		} else if p.token == token.String {
			v, _ := strconv.Unquote(p.tokenLit)
			name = v
		} else {
			p.errorExpected(pos, "map key")
		}
		p.next()

		var (
			value    Expr
			colonPos Pos
			def      Expr
		)
		if p.token == token.Colon {
			colonPos = p.pos
			p.next()
			value = p.parsePattern()
		} else {
			value = &Ident{Name: name, NamePos: pos}
		}
		if p.token == token.Assign {
			p.next()
			def = p.parseExpr()
		}

		fields = append(fields, &MapPatternField{
			Key:      name,
			KeyPos:   pos,
			ColonPos: colonPos,
			Value:    value,
			Default:  def,
		})

		if !p.expectComma(token.RBrace, "map pattern field") {
			break
		}
	}
	rbrace := p.expect(token.RBrace)
	return &MapPattern{
		LBrace: lbrace,
		RBrace: rbrace,
		Fields: fields,
	}
}

// isPatternLHS reports whether the bracketed expression currently being parsed
// is followed by an assignment operator (:= or =) and should therefore be
// parsed as a destructuring pattern.
func (p *Parser) isPatternLHS() bool {
	offset := p.scanner.offset
	s := NewScanner(p.file, p.scanner.src, nil, 0)
	s.offset = offset
	s.readOffset = offset
	s.lineOffset = offset
	s.ch = ' '
	s.next()

	depth := 1
	for {
		tok, _, _ := s.Scan()
		switch tok {
		case token.LBrack, token.LBrace, token.LParen:
			depth++
		case token.RBrack, token.RBrace, token.RParen:
			depth--
			if depth == 0 {
				tok2, _, _ := s.Scan()
				return tok2 == token.Define || tok2 == token.Assign
			}
		case token.EOF, token.Semicolon:
			return false
		}
	}
}
'''

assert old in s, "old parseIdentList not found"
s = s.replace(old, new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY
echo done

old='''func (c *Compiler) compileAssign(
	node parser.Node,
	lhs, rhs []parser.Expr,
	op token.Token,
) error {
	numLHS, numRHS := len(lhs), len(rhs)
	if numLHS > 1 || numRHS > 1 {
		return c.errorf(node, "tuple assignment not allowed")
	}

	// resolve and compile left-hand side
	ident, selectors := resolveAssignLHS(lhs[0])
	numSel := len(selectors)
'''
new='''func (c *Compiler) compileAssign(
	node parser.Node,
	lhs, rhs []parser.Expr,
	op token.Token,
) error {
	numLHS, numRHS := len(lhs), len(rhs)
	if numLHS > 1 || numRHS > 1 {
		return c.errorf(node, "tuple assignment not allowed")
	}

	// destructuring patterns
	switch lhs[0].(type) {
	case *parser.ArrayPattern, *parser.MapPattern:
		if op != token.Define {
			return c.errorf(node, "cannot use destructuring with =")
		}
		if err := c.Compile(rhs[0]); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return c.compilePattern(lhs[0], node)
	}

	// resolve and compile left-hand side
	ident, selectors := resolveAssignLHS(lhs[0])
	numSel := len(selectors)
'''

old='''	case *parser.FuncLit:
		c.enterScope()

		for _, p := range node.Type.Params.List {
			s := c.symbolTable.Define(p.Name)

			// function arguments is not assigned directly.
			s.LocalAssigned = true
		}

		if err := c.Compile(node.Body); err != nil {
			return err
		}
'''
new='''	case *parser.FuncLit:
		c.enterScope()

		var patternParams []struct {
			index   int
			pattern parser.Expr
		}
		for i, p := range node.Type.Params.List {
			if ident, ok := p.(*parser.Ident); ok {
				s := c.symbolTable.Define(ident.Name)

				// function arguments is not assigned directly.
				s.LocalAssigned = true
			} else {
				// A pattern parameter is stored in a temporary local slot
				// and destructured at the beginning of the function.
				s := c.symbolTable.Define(fmt.Sprintf("$param%d", i))
				s.LocalAssigned = true
				patternParams = append(patternParams, struct {
					index   int
					pattern parser.Expr
				}{index: s.Index, pattern: p})
			}
		}

		// Destructure pattern parameters before executing the body.
		for _, pp := range patternParams {
			c.emit(pp.pattern, parser.OpGetLocal, pp.index)
			if err := c.compilePattern(pp.pattern, pp.pattern); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}

		if err := c.Compile(node.Body); err != nil {
			return err
		}
'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY
echo done

p='compiler.go'
s=open(p).read()
marker='''func (c *Compiler) compileLogical(node *parser.BinaryExpr) error {
'''
insert='''func (c *Compiler) compilePattern(
	pattern parser.Expr,
	node parser.Node,
) error {
	switch pat := pattern.(type) {
	case *parser.ArrayPattern:
		return c.compileArrayPattern(pat, node)
	case *parser.MapPattern:
		return c.compileMapPattern(pat, node)
	case *parser.Ident:
		return c.compileIdentBinding(pat, node)
	default:
		return c.errorf(node, "invalid destructuring pattern")
	}
}

func (c *Compiler) compileArrayPattern(
	pattern *parser.ArrayPattern,
	node parser.Node,
) error {
	index := 0
	for _, elt := range pattern.Elements {
		if elt.Rest {
			ident, ok := elt.Value.(*parser.Ident)
			if !ok {
				return c.errorf(elt, "rest element must be last")
			}
			// source[ index: ] -> remaining elements
			c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)
			c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&Int{Value: int64(index)}))
			c.emit(node, parser.OpNull)
			c.emit(node, parser.OpSliceIndex)
			if err := c.compileBinding(ident, node); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			continue
		}

		c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)
		c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&Int{Value: int64(index)}))
		c.emit(node, parser.OpIndex)
		if elt.Default != nil {
			if err := c.compileDefaultBinding(elt.Value, elt.Default, node); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		} else {
			if err := c.compileBinding(elt.Value, node); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		index++
	}
	c.emit(node, parser.OpPop)
	return nil
}

func (c *Compiler) compileMapPattern(
	pattern *parser.MapPattern,
	node parser.Node,
) error {
	for _, field := range pattern.Fields {
		if len(field.Key) > MaxStringLen {
			return c.error(node, ErrStringLimit)
		}
		c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)
		c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&String{Value: field.Key}))
		c.emit(node, parser.OpIndex)
		if field.Default != nil {
			if err := c.compileDefaultBinding(field.Value, field.Default, node); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		} else {
			if err := c.compileBinding(field.Value, node); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	}
	c.emit(node, parser.OpPop)
	return nil
}

func (c *Compiler) compileBinding(
	value parser.Expr,
	node parser.Node,
) error {
	switch v := value.(type) {
	case *parser.Ident:
		return c.compileIdentBinding(v, node)
	case *parser.ArrayPattern:
		return c.compilePattern(v, node)
	case *parser.MapPattern:
		return c.compilePattern(v, node)
	default:
		return c.errorf(node, "invalid destructuring binding")
	}
}

func (c *Compiler) compileDefaultBinding(
	value parser.Expr,
	def parser.Expr,
	node parser.Node,
) error {
	// stack: [..., value]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)   // [..., value, value]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpNull)  // [..., value, value, undefined]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpEqual) // [..., value, bool]
	skipDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpFalsy, 0)
	c.emit(node, parser.OpPop) // [..., ]
	if err := c.Compile(def); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	end := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
	c.changeOperand(skipDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	if err := c.compileBinding(value, node); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.changeOperand(end, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	return nil
}

func (c *Compiler) compileIdentBinding(
	ident *parser.Ident,
	node parser.Node,
) error {
	if ident.Name == "_" {
		c.emit(node, parser.OpPop)
		return nil
	}

	symbol, depth, exists := c.symbolTable.Resolve(ident.Name, false)
	if depth == 0 && exists {
		return c.errorf(node, "'%s' redeclared in this block", ident.Name)
	}
	symbol = c.symbolTable.Define(ident.Name)

	switch symbol.Scope {
	case ScopeGlobal:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetGlobal, symbol.Index)
	case ScopeLocal:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpDefineLocal, symbol.Index)
		symbol.LocalAssigned = true
	case ScopeFree:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetFree, symbol.Index)
	default:
		panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid assignment variable scope: %s",
			symbol.Scope))
	}
	return nil
}

'''
assert marker in s
s=s.replace(marker, insert+marker)
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY
echo done
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
done

returncode=0
206:func (c *Compiled) Run() error {

package tengo

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
)

// Script can simplify compilation and execution of embedded scripts.
type Script struct {
	variables        map[string]*Variable
	modules          ModuleGetter
	input            []byte
	maxAllocs        int64
	maxConstObjects  int
	enableFileImport bool
	importDir        string
}

// NewScript creates a Script instance with an input script.
func NewScript(input []byte) *Script {
	return &Script{
		variables:       make(map[string]*Variable),
		input:           input,
		maxAllocs:       -1,
		maxConstObjects: -1,
	}
}

// Add adds a new variable or updates an existing variable to the script.
func (s *Script) Add(name string, value interface{}) error {
	obj, err := FromInterface(value)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	s.variables[name] = &Variable{
		name:  name,
		value: obj,
	}
	return nil
}

// Remove removes (undefines) an existing variable for the script. It returns
// false if the variable name is not defined.
func (s *Script) Remove(name string) bool {
	if _, ok := s.variables[name]; !ok {
		return false
	}
	delete(s.variables, name)
	return true
}

// SetImports sets import modules.
func (s *Script) SetImports(modules ModuleGetter) {
	s.modules = modules
}

// SetImportDir sets the initial import directory for script files.
func (s *Script) SetImportDir(dir string) error {
	dir, err := filepath.Abs(dir)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	s.importDir = dir
	return nil
}


END STEP

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
)

func run(name, src string) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	s.SetImports(nil)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Printf("%s: ERR %v\n", name, err)
		return
	}
	parts := ""
	for _, v := range c.GetAll() {
		if parts != "" { parts += ", " }
		parts += v.Name() + "=" + v.Value().String()
	}
	fmt.Printf("%s: OK {%s}\n", name, parts)
}

func main() {
	run("array", `[a, b] := [1, 2]`)
	run("map", `{x: a, y} := {x: 10, y: 20}`)
	run("rest", `[a, ...rest] := [1, 2, 3]`)
	run("default", `[a = 5] := []`)
	run("default_map", `{x: a = 50} := {}`)
	run("nested", `[[a, b], c] := [[1, 2], 3]`)
	run("empty", `[] := [1,2,3]`)
	run("missing", `[a, b] := [1]`)
	run("map_rename", `{x: a = 50} := {x: 10}`)
	run("ref_default", `[a, b = a + 1] := [10]`)
}

OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
package tengo

import (
	"errors"
)

// Variable is a user-defined variable for the script.
type Variable struct {
	name  string
	value Object
}

// NewVariable creates a Variable.
func NewVariable(name string, value interface{}) (*Variable, error) {
	obj, err := FromInterface(value)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &Variable{
		name:  name,
		value: obj,
	}, nil
}

// Name returns the name of the variable.
func (v *Variable) Name() string {
	return v.name
}

// Value returns an empty interface of the variable value.
func (v *Variable) Value() interface{} {
	return ToInterface(v.value)
}

// ValueType returns the name of the value type.
func (v *Variable) ValueType() string {
	return v.value.TypeName()
}

// Int returns int value of the variable value.
// It returns 0 if the value is not convertible to int.
func (v *Variable) Int() int {
	c, _ := ToInt(v.value)
	return c
}

// Int64 returns int64 value of the variable value. It returns 0 if the value
// is not convertible to int64.
func (v *Variable) Int64() int64 {
	c, _ := ToInt64(v.value)
	return c
}

// Float returns float64 value of the variable value. It returns 0.0 if the
// value is not convertible to float64.
func (v *Variable) Float() float64 {
	c, _ := ToFloat64(v.value)
	return c
}

// Char returns rune value of the variable value. It returns 0 if the value is
// not convertible to rune.
func (v *Variable) Char() rune {
	c, _ := ToRune(v.value)
	return c
}

// Bool returns bool value of the variable value. It returns 0 if the value is
// not convertible to bool.
func (v *Variable) Bool() bool {
	c, _ := ToBool(v.value)
	return c
}

// Array returns []interface value of the variable value. It returns 0 if the
// value is not convertible to []interface.
func (v *Variable) Array() []interface{} {
	switch val := v.value.(type) {
	case *Array:
		var arr []interface{}
		for _, e := range val.Value {
			arr = append(arr, ToInterface(e))
		}
		return arr
	}
	return nil
}

// Map returns map[string]interface{} value of the variable value. It returns
// 0 if the value is not convertible to map[string]interface{}.
func (v *Variable) Map() map[string]interface{} {
	switch val := v.value.(type) {
	case *Map:
		kv := make(map[string]interface{})
		for mk, mv := range val.Value {
			kv[mk] = ToInterface(mv)
		}
		return kv
	}
	return nil
}

// String returns string value of the variable value. It returns 0 if the value
// is not convertible to string.
func (v *Variable) String() string {
	c, _ := ToString(v.value)
	return c
}

// Bytes returns a byte slice of the variable value. It returns nil if the
// value is not convertible to byte slice.
func (v *Variable) Bytes() []byte {
	c, _ := ToByteSlice(v.value)
	return c
}

// Error returns an error if the underlying value is error object. If not,
// this returns nil.
func (v *Variable) Error() error {
	err, ok := v.value.(*Error)
	if ok {
		return errors.New(err.String())
	}
	return nil
}

// Object returns an underlying Object of the variable value. Note that
// returned Object is a copy of an actual Object used in the script.
func (v *Variable) Object() Object {
	return v.value
}

// IsUndefined returns true if the underlying value is undefined.
func (v *Variable) IsUndefined() bool {
	return v.value == UndefinedValue
}

END STEP

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
)

func main() {
	input := `[a = 5] := []`
	fileSet := parser.NewFileSet()
	file := fileSet.AddFile("test", -1, len(input))
	p := parser.NewParser(file, []byte(input), nil)
	parsed, err := p.ParseFile()
	if err != nil { fmt.Println("parse err:", err); return }
	symTable := tengo.NewSymbolTable()
	for idx, fn := range tengo.GetAllBuiltinFunctions() {
		symTable.DefineBuiltin(idx, fn.Name)
	}
	c := tengo.NewCompiler(file, symTable, nil, nil, nil)
	err = c.Compile(parsed)
	if err != nil { fmt.Println("compile err:", err); return }
	bc := c.Bytecode()
	bc.RemoveDuplicates()
	fmt.Println(strings.Join(bc.FormatInstructions(), "\n"))
}

   154	
   155	// RunContext is like Run but includes a context.
   156	func (s *Script) RunContext(
   157		ctx context.Context,
   158	) (compiled *Compiled, err error) {
   159		compiled, err = s.Compile()
   160		if err != nil {
   161			return
   162		}
   163		err = compiled.RunContext(ctx)
   164		return
   165	}
   166	
   167	func (s *Script) prepCompile() (
   168		symbolTable *SymbolTable,
   169		globals []Object,
   170		err error,
   171	) {
   172		var names []string
   173		for name := range s.variables {
   174			names = append(names, name)
   175		}
   176	
   177		symbolTable = NewSymbolTable()
   178		for idx, fn := range builtinFuncs {
   179			symbolTable.DefineBuiltin(idx, fn.Name)
   180		}
   181	
   182		globals = make([]Object, GlobalsSize)
   183	
   184		for idx, name := range names {
   185			symbol := symbolTable.Define(name)
   186			if symbol.Index != idx {
   187				panic(fmt.Errorf("wrong symbol index: %d != %d",
   188					idx, symbol.Index))
   189			}
   190			globals[symbol.Index] = s.variables[name].value
   191		}
   192		return
   193	}
   194	
   195	// Compiled is a compiled instance of the user script. Use Script.Compile() to
   196	// create Compiled object.
   197	type Compiled struct {
   198		globalIndexes map[string]int // global symbol name to index
   199		bytecode      *Bytecode
   200		globals       []Object
   201		maxAllocs     int64
   202		lock          sync.RWMutex
   203	}
   204	
   205	// Run executes the compiled script in the virtual machine.

END STEP

returncode=0
package tengo

import (
	"encoding/gob"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"reflect"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
)

// Bytecode is a compiled instructions and constants.
type Bytecode struct {
	FileSet      *parser.SourceFileSet
	MainFunction *CompiledFunction
	Constants    []Object
}

// Size of the bytecode in bytes
// (as much as we can calculate it without reflection and black magic)
func (b *Bytecode) Size() int64 {
	return b.MainFunction.Size() + b.FileSet.Size() + int64(len(b.Constants))
}

// Encode writes Bytecode data to the writer.
func (b *Bytecode) Encode(w io.Writer) error {
	enc := gob.NewEncoder(w)
	if err := enc.Encode(b.FileSet); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := enc.Encode(b.MainFunction); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return enc.Encode(b.Constants)
}

// CountObjects returns the number of objects found in Constants.
func (b *Bytecode) CountObjects() int {
	n := 0
	for _, c := range b.Constants {
		n += CountObjects(c)
	}
	return n
}

// FormatInstructions returns human readable string representations of
// compiled instructions.
func (b *Bytecode) FormatInstructions() []string {
	return FormatInstructions(b.MainFunction.Instructions, 0)
}

// FormatConstants returns human readable string representations of
// compiled constants.
func (b *Bytecode) FormatConstants() (output []string) {
	for cidx, cn := range b.Constants {
		switch cn := cn.(type) {
		case *CompiledFunction:
			output = append(output, fmt.Sprintf(
				"[% 3d] (Compiled Function|%p)", cidx, &cn))
			for _, l := range FormatInstructions(cn.Instructions, 0) {
				output = append(output, fmt.Sprintf("     %s", l))
			}
		default:
			output = append(output, fmt.Sprintf("[% 3d] %s (%s|%p)",
				cidx, cn, reflect.TypeOf(cn).Elem().Name(), &cn))
		}
	}
	return
}

// Decode reads Bytecode data from the reader.
func (b *Bytecode) Decode(r io.Reader, modules *ModuleMap) error {
	if modules == nil {
		modules = NewModuleMap()
	}

	dec := gob.NewDecoder(r)
	if err := dec.Decode(&b.FileSet); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	// TODO: files in b.FileSet.File does not have their 'set' field properly
	//  set to b.FileSet as it's private field and not serialized by gob
	//  encoder/decoder.
	if err := dec.Decode(&b.MainFunction); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := dec.Decode(&b.Constants); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for i, v := range b.Constants {
		fv, err := fixDecodedObject(v, modules)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		b.Constants[i] = fv
	}
	return nil
}

// RemoveDuplicates finds and remove the duplicate values in Constants.
// Note this function mutates Bytecode.
func (b *Bytecode) RemoveDuplicates() {
	var deduped []Object

	indexMap := make(map[int]int) // mapping from old constant index to new index
	fns := make(map[*CompiledFunction]int)
	ints := make(map[int64]int)
	strings := make(map[string]int)
	floats := make(map[float64]int)
	chars := make(map[rune]int)
	immutableMaps := make(map[string]int) // for modules

	for curIdx, c := range b.Constants {
		switch c := c.(type) {
		case *CompiledFunction:
			if newIdx, ok := fns[c]; ok {
				indexMap[curIdx] = newIdx
			} else {
				newIdx = len(deduped)
				fns[c] = newIdx

END STEP

	value parser.Expr,

	node parser.Node,
) error {
	// stack: [..., value]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)   // [..., value, value]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpNull)  // [..., value, value, undefined]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpEqual) // [..., value, bool]
	skipDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpFalsy, 0)
	c.emit(node, parser.OpPop) // [..., ]
	if err := c.Compile(def); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	end := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
	c.changeOperand(skipDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	if err := c.compileBinding(value, node); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.changeOperand(end, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	return nil
}

	value parser.Expr,
	def parser.Expr,
	node parser.Node,
) error {
	// stack: [..., value]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)   // [..., value, value]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpNull)  // [..., value, value, undefined]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpEqual) // [..., value, bool]
	skipDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpFalsy, 0)

old='''func (c *Compiler) compileDefaultBinding(
	value parser.Expr,
	def parser.Expr,
	node parser.Node,
) error {
	// stack: [..., value]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)   // [..., value, value]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpNull)  // [..., value, value, undefined]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpEqual) // [..., value, bool]
	skipDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpFalsy, 0)
	// fall-through when value == undefined
	c.emit(node, parser.OpPop) // [..., ]
	if err := c.Compile(def); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := c.compileBinding(value, node); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	end := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
	c.changeOperand(skipDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	// value != undefined path
	if err := c.compileBinding(value, node); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.changeOperand(end, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	return nil

func (c *Compiler) compileIdentBinding(
	ident *parser.Ident,
	node parser.Node,
) error {
	if ident.Name == "_" {
		c.emit(node, parser.OpPop)
		return nil
	}

	symbol, depth, exists := c.symbolTable.Resolve(ident.Name, false)
	if depth == 0 && exists {
		return c.errorf(node, "'%s' redeclared in this block", ident.Name)
	}
	symbol = c.symbolTable.Define(ident.Name)

	switch symbol.Scope {
	case ScopeGlobal:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetGlobal, symbol.Index)
	case ScopeLocal:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpDefineLocal, symbol.Index)
		symbol.LocalAssigned = true
	case ScopeFree:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetFree, symbol.Index)
	default:
		panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid assignment variable scope: %s",
			symbol.Scope))
	}
	return nil
}
'''
new='''func (c *Compiler) compileDefaultBinding(
	value parser.Expr,
	def parser.Expr,
	node parser.Node,
) error {
	ident, ok := value.(*parser.Ident)
	if !ok {
		return c.errorf(node, "default value only allowed on identifier binding")
	}

	symbol, err := c.declareIdentBinding(ident, node)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// stack: [..., value]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)   // [..., value, value]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpNull)  // [..., value, value, undefined]
	c.emit(node, parser.OpEqual) // [..., value, bool]
	skipDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpFalsy, 0)
	// fall-through when value == undefined
	c.emit(node, parser.OpPop) // [..., ]
	if err := c.Compile(def); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.emitSetIdent(ident, symbol, node)
	end := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
	c.changeOperand(skipDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	// value != undefined path
	c.emitSetIdent(ident, symbol, node)
	c.changeOperand(end, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	return nil
}

func (c *Compiler) compileIdentBinding(
	ident *parser.Ident,
	node parser.Node,
) error {
	symbol, err := c.declareIdentBinding(ident, node)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.emitSetIdent(ident, symbol, node)
	return nil
}

func (c *Compiler) declareIdentBinding(
	ident *parser.Ident,
	node parser.Node,
) (*Symbol, error) {
	if ident.Name == "_" {
		return nil, nil
	}

	symbol, depth, exists := c.symbolTable.Resolve(ident.Name, false)
	if depth == 0 && exists {
		return nil, c.errorf(node, "'%s' redeclared in this block", ident.Name)
	}
	return c.symbolTable.Define(ident.Name), nil
}

func (c *Compiler) emitSetIdent(
	ident *parser.Ident,
	symbol *Symbol,
	node parser.Node,
) {
	if ident.Name == "_" || symbol == nil {
		c.emit(node, parser.OpPop)
		return
	}

	switch symbol.Scope {
	case ScopeGlobal:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetGlobal, symbol.Index)
	case ScopeLocal:
		if !symbol.LocalAssigned {
			c.emit(node, parser.OpDefineLocal, symbol.Index)
			symbol.LocalAssigned = true
		} else {
			c.emit(node, parser.OpSetLocal, symbol.Index)
		}
	case ScopeFree:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetFree, symbol.Index)
	default:
		panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid assignment variable scope: %s",
			symbol.Scope))
	}
}
'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY

   951			return err
   952		}
   953		c.emitSetIdent(ident, symbol, node)
   954		return nil
   955	}
   956	
   957	func (c *Compiler) declareIdentBinding(
   958		ident *parser.Ident,
   959		node parser.Node,
   960	) (*Symbol, error) {
   961		if ident.Name == "_" {
   962			return nil, nil
   963		}
   964	
   965		symbol, depth, exists := c.symbolTable.Resolve(ident.Name, false)
   966		if depth == 0 && exists {
   967			return nil, c.errorf(node, "'%s' redeclared in this block", ident.Name)
   968		}
   969		return c.symbolTable.Define(ident.Name), nil
   970	}
   971	
   972	func (c *Compiler) emitSetIdent(
   973		ident *parser.Ident,
   974		symbol *Symbol,
   975		node parser.Node,
   976	) {
   977		if ident.Name == "_" || symbol == nil {
   978			c.emit(node, parser.OpPop)
   979			return
   980		}
   981	
   982		switch symbol.Scope {
   983		case ScopeGlobal:
   984			c.emit(node, parser.OpSetGlobal, symbol.Index)
   985		case ScopeLocal:
   986			if !symbol.LocalAssigned {
   987				c.emit(node, parser.OpDefineLocal, symbol.Index)
   988				symbol.LocalAssigned = true
   989			} else {
   990				c.emit(node, parser.OpSetLocal, symbol.Index)
   991			}
   992		case ScopeFree:
   993			c.emit(node, parser.OpSetFree, symbol.Index)
   994		default:
   995			panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid assignment variable scope: %s",
   996				symbol.Scope))
   997		}
   998	}
   999	
  1000	func (c *Compiler) compileLogical(node *parser.BinaryExpr) error {

END STEP

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
)

func run(name, src string) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	s.SetImports(nil)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Printf("%s: ERR %v\n", name, err)
		return
	}
	parts := ""
	for _, v := range c.GetAll() {
		if parts != "" { parts += ", " }
		parts += v.Name() + "=" + fmt.Sprintf("%v", v.Value())
	}
	fmt.Printf("%s: OK {%s}\n", name, parts)
}

func main() {
	run("fn_array", `f := func([a, b]) { return a + b }; out := f([1, 2])`)
	run("fn_map", `f := func({x: a, y}) { return a + y }; out := f({x: 10, y: 20})`)
	run("fn_nested", `f := func([[a], b]) { return a + b }; out := f([[5], 3])`)

}

  1254			elements = append(elements, p.parseMapElementLit())
  1255	
  1256			if !p.expectComma(token.RBrace, "map element") {
  1257				break
  1258			}
  1259		}
  1260	
  1261		p.exprLevel--
  1262		rbrace := p.expect(token.RBrace)
  1263		return &MapLit{
  1264			LBrace:   lbrace,
  1265			RBrace:   rbrace,
  1266			Elements: elements,
  1267		}
  1268	}

   107	func TestArray(t *testing.T) {
   108		expectRun(t, `out = [1, 2 * 2, 3 + 3]`, nil, ARR{1, 4, 6})
   109	
   110		// array copy-by-reference
   111		expectRun(t, `a1 := [1, 2, 3]; a2 := a1; a1[0] = 5; out = a2`,
   112			nil, ARR{5, 2, 3})
   113		expectRun(t, `func () { a1 := [1, 2, 3]; a2 := a1; a1[0] = 5; out = a2 }()`,
   114			nil, ARR{5, 2, 3})
   115	
   116		// array index set
   117		expectError(t, `a1 := [1, 2, 3]; a1[3] = 5`,
   118			nil, "index out of bounds")
   119	
   120		// index operator
   121		arr := ARR{1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6}
   122		arrStr := `[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]`
   123		arrLen := 6
   124		for idx := 0; idx < arrLen; idx++ {
   125			expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[%d]", arrStr, idx),
   126				nil, arr[idx])
   127			expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[0 + %d]", arrStr, idx),
   128				nil, arr[idx])
   129			expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[1 + %d - 1]", arrStr, idx),
   130				nil, arr[idx])
   131			expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("idx := %d; out = %s[idx]", idx, arrStr),
   132				nil, arr[idx])
   133		}
   134	
   135		expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("%s[%d]", arrStr, -1),
   136			nil, tengo.UndefinedValue)
   137		expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("%s[%d]", arrStr, arrLen),
   138			nil, tengo.UndefinedValue)
   139	
   140		// slice operator
   141		for low := 0; low < arrLen; low++ {
   142			expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[%d:%d]", arrStr, low, low),
   143				nil, ARR{})
   144			for high := low; high <= arrLen; high++ {
   145				expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[%d:%d]", arrStr, low, high),
   146					nil, arr[low:high])
   147				expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[0 + %d : 0 + %d]",
   148					arrStr, low, high), nil, arr[low:high])
   149				expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[1 + %d - 1 : 1 + %d - 1]",
   150					arrStr, low, high), nil, arr[low:high])
   151				expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[:%d]", arrStr, high),
   152					nil, arr[:high])
   153				expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[%d:]", arrStr, low),
   154					nil, arr[low:])
   155			}
   156		}
   157	
   158		expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[:]", arrStr),
   159			nil, arr)
   160		expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[%d:]", arrStr, -1),
   161			nil, arr)
   162		expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[:%d]", arrStr, arrLen+1),
   163			nil, arr)
   164		expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[%d:%d]", arrStr, 2, 2),
   165			nil, ARR{})
   166	
   167		expectError(t, fmt.Sprintf("%s[:%d]", arrStr, -1),
   168			nil, "invalid slice index")
   169		expectError(t, fmt.Sprintf("%s[%d:]", arrStr, arrLen+1),
   170			nil, "invalid slice index")

cat > /app/destructuring_test.go <<'EOF'
package tengo_test

import (
	"testing"
)

func TestDestructuringArray(t *testing.T) {
	// basic positional binding
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [a, b] := [1, 2]; out = a + b`, nil, 3)

	// positions beyond the array length bind undefined
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [a, b, c] := [1]; out = a; out = b; out = c`, nil, tengoUndefined())

	// empty pattern
	expectRun(t, `[] := [1, 2, 3]; out = 1`, nil, 1)

	// nested array patterns
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [[a, b], c] := [[1, 2], 3]; out = a + b + c`, nil, 6)

	// rest element
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [a, ...rest] := [1, 2, 3]; out = len(rest)`, nil, 2)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [a, ...rest] := [1, 2, 3]; out = rest[0] + rest[1]`, nil, 5)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [...rest] := [1, 2, 3]; out = len(rest)`, nil, 3)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [...rest] := []; out = len(rest)`, nil, 0)

	// defaults
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [a = 5] := []; out = a`, nil, 5)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [a = 5] := [2]; out = a`, nil, 2)

	// defaults reference earlier bindings
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [a, b = a + 1] := [10]; out = b`, nil, 11)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [a, b = a + 1] := [10, 20]; out = b`, nil, 20)
}

func TestDestructuringMap(t *testing.T) {
	// renaming
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; {x: a} := {x: 10}; out = a`, nil, 10)

	// shorthand
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; {x} := {x: 10}; out = x`, nil, 10)

	// shorthand with default
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; {x = 5} := {}; out = x`, nil, 5)

	// renaming with default
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; {x: a = 50} := {}; out = a`, nil, 50)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; {x: a = 50} := {x: 10}; out = a`, nil, 10)

	// absent key binds undefined
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; {x: a, y: b} := {x: 1}; out = b`, nil, tengoUndefined())

	// empty pattern
	expectRun(t, `{} := {a: 1}; out = 1`, nil, 1)

	// nested map patterns
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; {x: {a}} := {x: {a: 5}}; out = a`, nil, 5)

	// string keys
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; {"x": a} := {x: 42}; out = a`, nil, 42)
}

func TestDestructuringNested(t *testing.T) {
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [{x: a}, b] := [{x: 1}, 2]; out = a + b`, nil, 3)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; {x: [a, b]} := {x: [1, 2]}; out = a + b`, nil, 3)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; [[{y: a}]] := [[{y: 9}]]; out = a`, nil, 9)
}

func TestDestructuringFunctionParams(t *testing.T) {
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; f := func([a, b]) { return a + b }; out = f([1, 2])`, nil, 3)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; f := func({x: a, y}) { return a + y }; out = f({x: 10, y: 20})`, nil, 30)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; f := func([[a], b]) { return a + b }; out = f([[5], 3])`, nil, 8)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; f := func([a = 10]) { return a }; out = f([])`, nil, 10)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; f := func([a, ...rest]) { return len(rest) }; out = f([1, 2, 3])`, nil, 2)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; f := func([a], ...rest) { return a + len(rest) }; out = f([1], 2, 3)`, nil, 3)
	expectRun(t, `out := 0; f := func([a, b = a + 1]) { return b }; out = f([5])`, nil, 6)
}

func TestDestructuringErrors(t *testing.T) {
	expectCompileError(t, `[a, ...b, c] := [1, 2, 3]`, "rest element must be last")
	expectCompileError(t, `[a, b] = [1, 2]`, "cannot use destructuring with =")
	expectCompileError(t, `{x: a} = {x: 1}`, "cannot use destructuring with =")
	expectCompileError(t, `a := 1; [a, b] := [2, 3]`, "redeclared")
}

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
)

func run(name, src string) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	s.SetImports(nil)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil { fmt.Printf("%s: ERR %v\n", name, err); return }
	for _, v := range c.GetAll() {
		if v.Name() == "out" { fmt.Printf("%s: out=%v\n", name, v.Value()) }
	}
}

func main() {
	// default should NOT be evaluated when value exists (1/0 would error)
	run("lazy_ok", `out = 0; [a, b = 1 / 0] := [1, 2]; out = b`)
	// default SHOULD be evaluated when missing (1/0 errors)
	run("lazy_err", `out = 0; [a, b = 1 / 0] := [1]; out = b`)
}
EOF

func TestParseDestructuringPatterns(t *testing.T) {
	expectParse(t, `[a, b] := [1, 2]`, func(p pfn) []Stmt {
		return stmts(
			assignStmt(
				exprs(arrayPattern(p(1, 1), p(1, 8),
					arrayPatternElement(ident("a", p(1, 2)), false, NoPos, nil),
					arrayPatternElement(ident("b", p(1, 5)), false, NoPos, nil))),
				exprs(arrayLit(p(1, 12), p(1, 17),
					intLit(1, p(1, 13)),
					intLit(2, p(1, 16)))),
				token.Define,
				p(1, 10)))
	})

	expectParse(t, `{x: a, y} := {x: 1, y: 2}`, func(p pfn) []Stmt {
		return stmts(
			assignStmt(
				exprs(mapPattern(p(1, 1), p(1, 12),
					mapPatternField("x", p(1, 2), p(1, 3), ident("a", p(1, 5)), nil),
					mapPatternField("y", p(1, 8), NoPos, ident("y", p(1, 8)), nil))),
				exprs(mapLit(p(1, 16), p(1, 28),
					mapElementLit("x", p(1, 17), p(1, 18), intLit(1, p(1, 20))),
					mapElementLit("y", p(1, 23), p(1, 24), intLit(2, p(1, 26))))),
				token.Define,
				p(1, 14)))
	})

	expectParse(t, `[a, ...rest] := src`, func(p pfn) []Stmt {
		return stmts(
			assignStmt(
				exprs(arrayPattern(p(1, 1), p(1, 13),
					arrayPatternElement(ident("a", p(1, 2)), false, NoPos, nil),
					arrayPatternElement(ident("rest", p(1, 9)), true, p(1, 6), nil))),
				exprs(ident("src", p(1, 17))),
				token.Define,
				p(1, 15)))
	})

	expectParse(t, `[a = 5] := src`, func(p pfn) []Stmt {
		return stmts(
			assignStmt(
				exprs(arrayPattern(p(1, 1), p(1, 8),
					arrayPatternElement(ident("a", p(1, 2)), false, NoPos,
						intLit(5, p(1, 6))))),
				exprs(ident("src", p(1, 12))),
				token.Define,
				p(1, 10)))
	})

	expectParse(t, `{x: a = 50} := src`, func(p pfn) []Stmt {
		return stmts(
			assignStmt(
				exprs(mapPattern(p(1, 1), p(1, 12),
					mapPatternField("x", p(1, 2), p(1, 3), ident("a", p(1, 5)),
						intLit(50, p(1, 9))))),
				exprs(ident("src", p(1, 16))),
				token.Define,
				p(1, 14)))
	})

	expectParse(t, `f := func([a, {x: b}]) {}`, func(p pfn) []Stmt {
		return stmts(
			assignStmt(
				exprs(ident("f", p(1, 1))),
				exprs(funcLit(
					funcType(
						identList(p(1, 10), p(1, 22), false,
							arrayPattern(p(1, 11), p(1, 21),
								arrayPatternElement(ident("a", p(1, 12)), false, NoPos, nil),
								arrayPatternElement(mapPattern(p(1, 15), p(1, 20),
									mapPatternField("x", p(1, 16), p(1, 17),
										ident("b", p(1, 19)), nil)), false, NoPos, nil))),
						p(1, 5)),
					blockStmt(p(1, 24), p(1, 25)))),
				token.Define,
				p(1, 3)))
	})

	expectParseError(t, `[a, ...b, c] := src`)
	expectParseError(t, `{...r} := src`)
}
EOF

    15	// AssignStmt represents an assignment statement.
    16	type AssignStmt struct {
    17		LHS      []Expr
    18		RHS      []Expr
    19		Token    token.Token
    20		TokenPos Pos
    21	}
    22	
    23	func (s *AssignStmt) stmtNode() {}
    24	
    25	// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
    26	func (s *AssignStmt) Pos() Pos {
    27		return s.LHS[0].Pos()
    28	}
    29	
    30	// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
    31	func (s *AssignStmt) End() Pos {
    32		return s.RHS[len(s.RHS)-1].End()
    33	}
    34	
    35	func (s *AssignStmt) String() string {
    36		var lhs, rhs []string
    37		for _, e := range s.LHS {
    38			lhs = append(lhs, e.String())
    39		}
    40		for _, e := range s.RHS {
    41			rhs = append(rhs, e.String())
    42		}
    43		return strings.Join(lhs, ", ") + " " + s.Token.String() +
    44			" " + strings.Join(rhs, ", ")
    45	}
    46	
    47	// BadStmt represents a bad statement.
    48	type BadStmt struct {
    49		From Pos
    50		To   Pos
    51	}
    52	
    53	func (s *BadStmt) stmtNode() {}

    55	// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
    56	func (s *BadStmt) Pos() Pos {
    57		return s.From
    58	}

STEP 116 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat >> parser/parser_test.go <<'EOF'

func TestParseDestructuringString(t *testing.T) {
	expectParseString(t, `[a, b] := [1, 2]`, `[a, b] := [1, 2]`)
	expectParseString(t, `{x: a, y} := {x: 1, y: 2}`, `{x: a, y} := {x: 1, y: 2}`)
	expectParseString(t, `[a, ...rest] := src`, `[a, ...rest] := src`)
	expectParseString(t, `[a = 5] := src`, `[a = 5] := src`)
	expectParseString(t, `{x: a = 50} := src`, `{x: a = 50} := src`)
	expectParseString(t, `{x = 5} := src`, `{x = 5} := src`)
	expectParseString(t, `[[a, b], c] := src`, `[[a, b], c] := src`)
	expectParseString(t, `{x: [a, b]} := src`, `{x: [a, b]} := src`)
	expectParseString(t, `f := func([a, {x: b}]) {}`, `f := func([a, {x: b}]) {}`)
	expectParseString(t, `f := func([a, ...rest]) {}`, `f := func([a, ...rest]) {}`)

	expectParseError(t, `[a, ...b, c] := src`)
	expectParseError(t, `{...r} := src`)
}
EOF
cd /app && go test ./parser/... -run TestParseDestructuringString -count=1 2>&1 | tail -20
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
ok  	github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser	0.004s

END STEP

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
)

func run(name, src string) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	s.SetImports(nil)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil { fmt.Printf("%s: ERR %v\n", name, err); return }
	parts := ""
	for _, v := range c.GetAll() {
		if parts != "" { parts += ", " }
		parts += v.Name() + "=" + fmt.Sprintf("%v", v.Value())
	}
	fmt.Printf("%s: OK {%s}\n", name, parts)
}

func main() {
	run("if_init", `out = 0; if [a, b] := [1, 2]; a == 1 { out = b }`)
	run("if_body", `out = 0; if true { {x: a} := {x: 9}; out = a }`)
	run("clojure_capture", `out = 0; f := func() { [a] := [7]; return func() { return a } }; out = f()()`)
}
EOF

				v.ip++
				start := int(v.curInsts[v.ip])
				left := v.stack[v.sp-1]
				v.sp--
				switch left := left.(type) {
				case *Array:
					if start >= len(left.Value) {
						start = len(left.Value)
					} else if start < 0 {
						start = 0
					}
					var val Object = &Array{Value: left.Value[start:]}
					v.allocs--
					if v.allocs == 0 {
						v.err = ErrObjectAllocLimit
						return
					}

					v.sp++

					} else if start < 0 {
						start = 0

					return
				}

				numArgs := int(v.curInsts[v.ip+1])
'''

package main

import (
	"fmt"

func run(name, src string) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	s.SetImports(nil)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil { fmt.Printf("%s: ERR %v\n", name, err); return }
	parts := ""
	for _, v := range c.GetAll() {
		if parts != "" { parts += ", " }

+func (c *Compiler) compilePattern(
+	pattern parser.Expr,
+	node parser.Node,
+) error {
+	switch pat := pattern.(type) {
+	case *parser.ArrayPattern:
+		return c.compileArrayPattern(pat, node)
+	case *parser.MapPattern:
+		return c.compileMapPattern(pat, node)
+	case *parser.Ident:
+		return c.compileIdentBinding(pat, node)
+	default:
+		return c.errorf(node, "invalid destructuring pattern")
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *Compiler) compileArrayPattern(
+	pattern *parser.ArrayPattern,
+	node parser.Node,
+) error {
+	index := 0
+	for _, elt := range pattern.Elements {
+		if elt.Rest {
+			ident, ok := elt.Value.(*parser.Ident)
+			if !ok {
+				return c.errorf(pattern, "rest element must be last")
+			}
+			// source[ index: ] -> remaining elements
+			c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)
+			c.emit(node, parser.OpArrayRest, index)
+			if err := c.compileBinding(ident, node); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+
+		c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)
+		c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&Int{Value: int64(index)}))
+		c.emit(node, parser.OpIndex)
+		if elt.Default != nil {
+			if err := c.compileDefaultBinding(elt.Value, elt.Default, node); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		} else {
+			if err := c.compileBinding(elt.Value, node); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		index++
+	}
+	c.emit(node, parser.OpPop)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (c *Compiler) compileMapPattern(
+	pattern *parser.MapPattern,
+	node parser.Node,
+) error {
+	for _, field := range pattern.Fields {
+		if len(field.Key) > MaxStringLen {
+			return c.error(node, ErrStringLimit)
+		}
+		c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)
+		c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&String{Value: field.Key}))
+		c.emit(node, parser.OpIndex)
+		if field.Default != nil {
+			if err := c.compileDefaultBinding(field.Value, field.Default, node); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		} else {
+			if err := c.compileBinding(field.Value, node); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	c.emit(node, parser.OpPop)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (c *Compiler) compileBinding(
+	value parser.Expr,
+	node parser.Node,
+) error {
+	switch v := value.(type) {
+	case *parser.Ident:
+		return c.compileIdentBinding(v, node)
+	case *parser.ArrayPattern:
+		return c.compilePattern(v, node)
+	case *parser.MapPattern:
+		return c.compilePattern(v, node)
+	default:
+		return c.errorf(node, "invalid destructuring binding")
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *Compiler) compileDefaultBinding(
+	value parser.Expr,
+	def parser.Expr,
+	node parser.Node,
+) error {
+	ident, ok := value.(*parser.Ident)
+	if !ok {
+		return c.errorf(node, "default value only allowed on identifier binding")
+	}
+
+	symbol, err := c.declareIdentBinding(ident, node)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	// stack: [..., value]
+	c.emit(node, parser.OpDup)   // [..., value, value]
+	c.emit(node, parser.OpNull)  // [..., value, value, undefined]
+	c.emit(node, parser.OpEqual) // [..., value, bool]
+	skipDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpFalsy, 0)
+	// fall-through when value == undefined
+	c.emit(node, parser.OpPop) // [..., ]
+	if err := c.Compile(def); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	c.emitSetIdent(ident, symbol, node)
+	end := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
+	c.changeOperand(skipDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
+	// value != undefined path
+	c.emitSetIdent(ident, symbol, node)
+	c.changeOperand(end, len(c.currentInstructions()))
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (c *Compiler) compileIdentBinding(
+	ident *parser.Ident,
+	node parser.Node,
+) error {
+	symbol, err := c.declareIdentBinding(ident, node)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	c.emitSetIdent(ident, symbol, node)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (c *Compiler) declareIdentBinding(
+	ident *parser.Ident,
+	node parser.Node,
+) (*Symbol, error) {
+	if ident.Name == "_" {
+		return nil, nil
+	}
+
+	_, depth, exists := c.symbolTable.Resolve(ident.Name, false)
+	if depth == 0 && exists {
+		return nil, c.errorf(node, "'%s' redeclared in this block", ident.Name)
+	}
+	return c.symbolTable.Define(ident.Name), nil
+}
+
+func (c *Compiler) emitSetIdent(
+	ident *parser.Ident,
+	symbol *Symbol,
+	node parser.Node,
+) {
+	if ident.Name == "_" || symbol == nil {
+		c.emit(node, parser.OpPop)
+		return
+	}
+
+	switch symbol.Scope {
+	case ScopeGlobal:
+		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetGlobal, symbol.Index)
+	case ScopeLocal:
+		if !symbol.LocalAssigned {
+			c.emit(node, parser.OpDefineLocal, symbol.Index)
+			symbol.LocalAssigned = true
+		} else {
+			c.emit(node, parser.OpSetLocal, symbol.Index)
+		}
+	case ScopeFree:
+		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetFree, symbol.Index)
+	default:
+		panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid assignment variable scope: %s",
+			symbol.Scope))
+	}
+}
+

 	// left side term
 	if err := c.Compile(node.LHS); err != nil {

+// parsePatternParam parses a function parameter, which can be an identifier,
+// an array pattern, or a map pattern.
+func (p *Parser) parsePatternParam() Expr {
+	switch p.token {
+	case token.LBrack:
+		return p.parseArrayPattern()
+	case token.LBrace:
+		return p.parseMapPattern()
+	default:
+		return p.parseIdent()
+	}
+}
+
+// parsePattern parses a destructuring pattern.
+func (p *Parser) parsePattern() Expr {
+	switch p.token {
+	case token.LBrack:
+		return p.parseArrayPattern()
+	case token.LBrace:
+		return p.parseMapPattern()
+	default:
+		return p.parseIdent()
+	}
+}
+
+// parseArrayPattern parses an array destructuring pattern.
+func (p *Parser) parseArrayPattern() Expr {
+	if p.trace {
+		defer untracep(tracep(p, "ArrayPattern"))
+	}
+
+	lbrack := p.expect(token.LBrack)
+	var elements []*ArrayPatternElement
+	for p.token != token.RBrack && p.token != token.EOF {
+		var (
+			rest        bool
+			ellipsisPos Pos
+		)
+		if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
+			rest = true
+			ellipsisPos = p.pos
+			p.next()
+		}
+
+		value := p.parsePattern()
+		var def Expr
+		if p.token == token.Assign {
+			if rest {
+				p.error(p.pos, "default value not allowed in rest element")
+			}
+			if _, ok := value.(*Ident); !ok {
+				p.error(p.pos, "default value only allowed on identifier binding")
+			}
+			p.next()
+			def = p.parseExpr()
+		}
+
+		elements = append(elements, &ArrayPatternElement{
+			Value:       value,
+			Rest:        rest,
+			EllipsisPos: ellipsisPos,
+			Default:     def,
+		})
+
+		if rest && p.token == token.Comma {
+			p.error(p.pos, "rest element must be last")
+		}
+		if !p.expectComma(token.RBrack, "array pattern element") {
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	rbrack := p.expect(token.RBrack)
+	return &ArrayPattern{
+		LBrack:   lbrack,
+		RBrack:   rbrack,
+		Elements: elements,
+	}
+}
+
+// parseMapPattern parses a map destructuring pattern.
+func (p *Parser) parseMapPattern() Expr {
+	if p.trace {
+		defer untracep(tracep(p, "MapPattern"))
+	}
+
+	lbrace := p.expect(token.LBrace)
+	var fields []*MapPatternField
+	for p.token != token.RBrace && p.token != token.EOF {
+		pos := p.pos
+		name := "_"
+		if p.token == token.Ident {
+			name = p.tokenLit
+		} else if p.token == token.String {
+			v, _ := strconv.Unquote(p.tokenLit)
+			name = v
+		} else {
+			p.errorExpected(pos, "map key")
+		}
+		p.next()
+
+		var (
+			value    Expr
+			colonPos Pos
+			def      Expr
+		)
+		if p.token == token.Colon {
+			colonPos = p.pos
+			p.next()
+			value = p.parsePattern()
+		} else {
+			value = &Ident{Name: name, NamePos: pos}
+		}
+		if p.token == token.Assign {
+			if _, ok := value.(*Ident); !ok {
+				p.error(p.pos, "default value only allowed on identifier binding")
+			}
+			p.next()
+			def = p.parseExpr()
+		}
+
+		fields = append(fields, &MapPatternField{
+			Key:      name,
+			KeyPos:   pos,
+			ColonPos: colonPos,
+			Value:    value,
+			Default:  def,
+		})
+
+		if !p.expectComma(token.RBrace, "map pattern field") {
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	rbrace := p.expect(token.RBrace)
+	return &MapPattern{
+		LBrace: lbrace,
+		RBrace: rbrace,
+		Fields: fields,
+	}
+}
+
+// isPatternLHS reports whether the bracketed expression currently being parsed
+// is followed by an assignment operator (:= or =) and should therefore be
+// parsed as a destructuring pattern.
+func (p *Parser) isPatternLHS() bool {
+	offset := p.scanner.offset
+	s := NewScanner(p.file, p.scanner.src, nil, 0)
+	s.offset = offset
+	s.readOffset = offset
+	s.lineOffset = offset
+	s.ch = ' '
+	s.next()
+
+	depth := 1
+	for {
+		tok, _, _ := s.Scan()
+		switch tok {
+		case token.LBrack, token.LBrace, token.LParen:
+			depth++
+		case token.RBrack, token.RBrace, token.RParen:
+			depth--
+			if depth == 0 {
+				tok2, _, _ := s.Scan()
+				return tok2 == token.Define || tok2 == token.Assign
+			}
+		case token.EOF:
+			return false
+		case token.Semicolon:
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return false
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}
+

 }
 
+func (p *Parser) parseLHSExprList() (list []Expr) {
+	if p.trace {
+		defer untracep(tracep(p, "LHSExpressionList"))
+	}
+
+	if (p.token == token.LBrack || p.token == token.LBrace) &&
+		p.isPatternLHS() {
+		list = append(list, p.parsePattern())
+		for p.token == token.Comma {
+			p.next()
+			list = append(list, p.parsePattern())
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	return p.parseExprList()
+}

 	if p.trace {
 		defer untracep(tracep(p, "ExpressionList"))

package main

	"fmt"

func run(name, src string) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	s.SetImports(nil)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil { fmt.Printf("%s: ERR %v\n", name, err); return }

}

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
)

func run(name, src string) {
	s := tengo.NewScript([]byte(src))
	s.SetImports(nil)
	c, err := s.Run()
	if err != nil { fmt.Printf("%s: ERR %v\n", name, err); return }
	parts := ""
	for _, v := range c.GetAll() {
		if parts != "" { parts += ", " }
		parts += v.Name() + "=" + fmt.Sprintf("%v", v.Value())
	}
	fmt.Printf("%s: OK {%s}\n", name, parts)
}

func main() {
	run("multi_line_stmt", "a := [1,2]\nb := 3")
	run("pattern_multi", "[a,b] := [1,2]\nc := 3")
}
EOF

OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
package parser

import (
	"strings"
)

// ArrayPattern represents an array destructuring pattern.
type ArrayPattern struct {
	LBrack   Pos
	Elements []*ArrayPatternElement
	RBrack   Pos
}

func (e *ArrayPattern) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *ArrayPattern) Pos() Pos {
	return e.LBrack
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *ArrayPattern) End() Pos {
	return e.RBrack + 1
}

func (e *ArrayPattern) String() string {
	var elements []string
	for _, elt := range e.Elements {
		elements = append(elements, elt.String())
	}
	return "[" + strings.Join(elements, ", ") + "]"
}

// ArrayPatternElement represents an element of an array pattern.
type ArrayPatternElement struct {
	Value       Expr
	Rest        bool
	EllipsisPos Pos
	Default     Expr
}

func (e *ArrayPatternElement) String() string {
	var s string
	if e.Rest {
		s = "..."
	}
	s += e.Value.String()
	if e.Default != nil {
		s += " = " + e.Default.String()
	}
	return s
}

// MapPattern represents a map destructuring pattern.
type MapPattern struct {
	LBrace Pos
	Fields []*MapPatternField
	RBrace Pos
}

func (e *MapPattern) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *MapPattern) Pos() Pos {
	return e.LBrace
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *MapPattern) End() Pos {
	return e.RBrace + 1
}

func (e *MapPattern) String() string {
	var fields []string
	for _, f := range e.Fields {
		fields = append(fields, f.String())
	}
	return "{" + strings.Join(fields, ", ") + "}"
}

// MapPatternField represents a field of a map pattern.
type MapPatternField struct {
	Key      string
	KeyPos   Pos
	ColonPos Pos
	Value    Expr
	Default  Expr
}

func (e *MapPatternField) String() string {
	var s string
	if e.ColonPos.IsValid() {
		s = e.Key + ": " + e.Value.String()
	} else {
		s = e.Value.String()
	}
	if e.Default != nil {
		s += " = " + e.Default.String()
	}
	return s
}

END STEP

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
)

func main() {
	for _, input := range []string{`f := func([a]) {}`, `f := func([a, ...rest]) {}`} {
		fileSet := parser.NewFileSet()
		file := fileSet.AddFile("test", -1, len(input))
		p := parser.NewParser(file, []byte(input), nil)
		parsed, err := p.ParseFile()
		if err != nil { fmt.Println("parse err:", err); continue }
		symTable := tengo.NewSymbolTable()
		for idx, fn := range tengo.GetAllBuiltinFunctions() { symTable.DefineBuiltin(idx, fn.Name) }
		c := tengo.NewCompiler(file, symTable, nil, nil, nil)
		err = c.Compile(parsed)
		if err != nil { fmt.Println("compile err:", err); continue }
		bc := c.Bytecode()
		bc.RemoveDuplicates()
		fmt.Println("=== ", input)
		fmt.Println(strings.Join(bc.FormatInstructions(), "\n"))
		for _, co := range bc.Constants {
			if f, ok := co.(*tengo.CompiledFunction); ok {
				fmt.Println("--- function:")
				fmt.Println(strings.Join(tengo.FormatInstructions(f.Instructions, 0), "\n"))
			}
		}
	}
}
EOF

   652	// SetImportFileExt sets the extension name of the source file for loading
   653	// local module files.
   654	//
   655	// Use this method if you want other source file extension than ".tengo".
   656	//
   657	//	// this will search for *.tengo, *.foo, *.bar
   658	//	err := c.SetImportFileExt(".tengo", ".foo", ".bar")
   659	//
   660	// This function requires at least one argument, since it will replace the
   661	// current list of extension name.
   662	func (c *Compiler) SetImportFileExt(exts ...string) error {
   663		if len(exts) == 0 {
   664			return fmt.Errorf("missing arg: at least one argument is required")
   665		}
   666	
   667		for _, ext := range exts {
   668			if ext != filepath.Ext(ext) || ext == "" {
   669				return fmt.Errorf("invalid file extension: %s", ext)
   670			}
   671		}
   672	
   673		c.importFileExt = exts // Replace the hole current extension list
   674	
   675		return nil
   676	}

   679	// Thease are the complementary suffix of the source file to search and load
   680	// local module files.

STEP 155 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='parser/parser.go'
s=open(p).read()
old='''	var params []Expr
	lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
	isVarArgs := false
	if p.token != token.RParen {
		if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
			isVarArgs = true
			p.next()
		}

		params = append(params, p.parsePatternParam())
		for !isVarArgs && p.token == token.Comma {
			p.next()
			if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
				isVarArgs = true
				p.next()
			}
			params = append(params, p.parsePatternParam())
		}
	}
'''
new='''	var params []Expr
	lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
	isVarArgs := false
	if p.token != token.RParen {
		if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
			isVarArgs = true
			p.next()
		}

		if isVarArgs {
			params = append(params, p.parseIdent())
		} else {
			params = append(params, p.parsePatternParam())
		}
		for !isVarArgs && p.token == token.Comma {
			p.next()
			if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
				isVarArgs = true
				p.next()
			}
			if isVarArgs {
				params = append(params, p.parseIdent())
			} else {
				params = append(params, p.parsePatternParam())
			}
		}
	}
'''

	for _, elt := range pattern.Elements {

			ident, ok := elt.Value.(*parser.Ident)
			if !ok {
				return c.errorf(pattern, "rest element must be last")
			}

	for i, elt := range pattern.Elements {
		if elt.Rest {
			if i != len(pattern.Elements)-1 {
				return c.errorf(pattern, "rest element must be last")
			}
			ident, ok := elt.Value.(*parser.Ident)
