STEP 163 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat destructuring_test.go
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
package tengo_test

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
)

func TestDestructuringArray(t *testing.T) {
	// basic positional binding
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [a, b] := [1, 2]; out = a + b`, nil, 3)

	// positions beyond the array length bind undefined
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [a, b, c] := [1]; out = a; out = b; out = c`, nil, tengo.UndefinedValue)

	// empty pattern
	expectRun(t, `[] := [1, 2, 3]; out = 1`, nil, 1)

	// nested array patterns
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [[a, b], c] := [[1, 2], 3]; out = a + b + c`, nil, 6)

	// rest element
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [a, ...rest] := [1, 2, 3]; out = len(rest)`, nil, 2)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [a, ...rest] := [1, 2, 3]; out = rest[0] + rest[1]`, nil, 5)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [...rest] := [1, 2, 3]; out = len(rest)`, nil, 3)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [...rest] := []; out = len(rest)`, nil, 0)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [a, b, ...rest] := [1]; out = len(rest)`, nil, 0)

	// defaults
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [a = 5] := []; out = a`, nil, 5)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [a = 5] := [2]; out = a`, nil, 2)

	// defaults reference earlier bindings
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [a, b = a + 1] := [10]; out = b`, nil, 11)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [a, b = a + 1] := [10, 20]; out = b`, nil, 20)
}

func TestDestructuringMap(t *testing.T) {
	// renaming
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; {x: a} := {x: 10}; out = a`, nil, 10)

	// shorthand
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; {x} := {x: 10}; out = x`, nil, 10)

	// shorthand with default
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; {x = 5} := {}; out = x`, nil, 5)

	// renaming with default
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; {x: a = 50} := {}; out = a`, nil, 50)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; {x: a = 50} := {x: 10}; out = a`, nil, 10)

	// absent key binds undefined
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; {x: a, y: b} := {x: 1}; out = b`, nil, tengo.UndefinedValue)

	// empty pattern
	expectRun(t, `{} := {a: 1}; out = 1`, nil, 1)

	// nested map patterns
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; {x: {a}} := {x: {a: 5}}; out = a`, nil, 5)

	// string keys
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; {"x": a} := {x: 42}; out = a`, nil, 42)
}

func TestDestructuringNested(t *testing.T) {
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [{x: a}, b] := [{x: 1}, 2]; out = a + b`, nil, 3)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; {x: [a, b]} := {x: [1, 2]}; out = a + b`, nil, 3)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; [[{y: a}]] := [[{y: 9}]]; out = a`, nil, 9)
}

func TestDestructuringFunctionParams(t *testing.T) {
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; f := func([a, b]) { return a + b }; out = f([1, 2])`, nil, 3)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; f := func({x: a, y}) { return a + y }; out = f({x: 10, y: 20})`, nil, 30)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; f := func([[a], b]) { return a + b }; out = f([[5], 3])`, nil, 8)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; f := func([a = 10]) { return a }; out = f([])`, nil, 10)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; f := func([a, ...rest]) { return len(rest) }; out = f([1, 2, 3])`, nil, 2)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; f := func([a, ...rest]) { return len(rest) }; out = f([1])`, nil, 0)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; f := func([a], ...rest) { return a + len(rest) }; out = f([1], 2, 3)`, nil, 3)
	expectRun(t, `out = 0; f := func([a, b = a + 1]) { return b }; out = f([5])`, nil, 6)
}

func TestDestructuringErrors(t *testing.T) {
	expectCompileError(t, `[a, ...b, c] := [1, 2, 3]`, "rest element must be last")
	expectCompileError(t, `[a, b] = [1, 2]`, "cannot use destructuring with =")
	expectCompileError(t, `{x: a} = {x: 1}`, "cannot use destructuring with =")
	expectCompileError(t, `a := 1; [a, b] := [2, 3]`, "redeclared")
}

END STEP

   249	
   250	func updateConstIndexes(insts []byte, indexMap map[int]int) {
   251		i := 0
   252		for i < len(insts) {
   253			op := insts[i]
   254			numOperands := parser.OpcodeOperands[op]
   255			_, read := parser.ReadOperands(numOperands, insts[i+1:])
   256	
   257			switch op {
   258			case parser.OpConstant:
   259				curIdx := int(insts[i+2]) | int(insts[i+1])<<8
   260				newIdx, ok := indexMap[curIdx]
   261				if !ok {
   262					panic(fmt.Errorf("constant index not found: %d", curIdx))
   263				}
   264				copy(insts[i:], MakeInstruction(op, newIdx))
   265			case parser.OpClosure:
   266				curIdx := int(insts[i+2]) | int(insts[i+1])<<8
   267				numFree := int(insts[i+3])
   268				newIdx, ok := indexMap[curIdx]
   269				if !ok {
   270					panic(fmt.Errorf("constant index not found: %d", curIdx))
   271				}
   272				copy(insts[i:], MakeInstruction(op, newIdx, numFree))
   273			}
   274	
   275			i += 1 + read
   276		}
   277	}
   278	
   279	func inferModuleName(mod *ImmutableMap) string {
   280		if modName, ok := mod.Value["__module_name__"].(*String); ok {
   281			return modName.Value
   282		}
   283		return ""
   284	}

   231		if s.readOffset < len(s.src) {
   232			s.offset = s.readOffset
   233			if s.ch == '\n' {
   234				s.lineOffset = s.offset
   235				s.file.AddLine(s.offset)
   236			}
   237			r, w := rune(s.src[s.readOffset]), 1

   239			case r == 0:
   240				s.error(s.offset, "illegal character NUL")
   241			case r >= utf8.RuneSelf:
   242				// not ASCII
   243				r, w = utf8.DecodeRune(s.src[s.readOffset:])
   244				if r == utf8.RuneError && w == 1 {
   245					s.error(s.offset, "illegal UTF-8 encoding")
   246				} else if r == bom && s.offset > 0 {
   247					s.error(s.offset, "illegal byte order mark")
   248				}
   249			}
   250			s.readOffset += w
   251			s.ch = r
   252		} else {
   253			s.offset = len(s.src)
   254			if s.ch == '\n' {
   255				s.lineOffset = s.offset
   256				s.file.AddLine(s.offset)
   257			}
   258			s.ch = -1 // eof
   259		}
   260	}

  1409		intIdx, ok := index.(*Int)
  1410		if !ok {

  1412			return
  1413		}

  1419			res = UndefinedValue
  1420			return
  1421		}

  1423		return
  1424	}

STEP 194 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='parser/ast.go'
s=open(p).read()
old='''// IdentList represents a list of parameters (identifiers or patterns).
type IdentList struct {
	LParen  Pos
	VarArgs bool
	List    []Expr
	RParen  Pos
}'''
new='''// IdentList represents a list of parameters (identifiers or patterns).
type IdentList struct {
	LParen  Pos
	VarArgs bool
	List    []*Ident
	// Patterns holds the pattern parameters that correspond to entries in
	// List (nil for plain identifier parameters).
	Patterns []Expr
	RParen   Pos
}'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
# Update End to handle patterns
old2='''// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (n *IdentList) End() Pos {
	if n.RParen.IsValid() {
		return n.RParen + 1
	}
	if l := len(n.List); l > 0 {
		return n.List[l-1].End()
	}
	return NoPos
}'''
new2='''// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (n *IdentList) End() Pos {
	if n.RParen.IsValid() {
		return n.RParen + 1
	}
	if l := len(n.List); l > 0 {
		if l <= len(n.Patterns) && n.Patterns[l-1] != nil {
			return n.Patterns[l-1].End()
		}
		return n.List[l-1].End()
	}
	return NoPos
}'''
assert old2 in s
s=s.replace(old2,new2)
# Update String to use Patterns
old3='''func (n *IdentList) String() string {
	var list []string
	for i, e := range n.List {
		if n.VarArgs && i == len(n.List)-1 {
			list = append(list, "..."+e.String())
		} else {
			list = append(list, e.String())
		}
	}
	return "(" + strings.Join(list, ", ") + ")"
}'''
new3='''func (n *IdentList) String() string {
	var list []string
	for i, e := range n.List {
		var s string
		if i < len(n.Patterns) && n.Patterns[i] != nil {
			s = n.Patterns[i].String()
		} else {
			s = e.String()
		}
		if n.VarArgs && i == len(n.List)-1 {
			list = append(list, "..."+s)
		} else {
			list = append(list, s)
		}
	}
	return "(" + strings.Join(list, ", ") + ")"
}'''
assert old3 in s
s=s.replace(old3,new3)
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY
echo done
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
done

END STEP

package parser_test

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
)

func TestIdentListString(t *testing.T) {
	identListVar := &parser.IdentList{
		List: []*parser.Ident{
			{Name: "a"},
			{Name: "b"},
			{Name: "c"},
		},
		VarArgs: true,
	}

	expectedVar := "(a, b, ...c)"
	if str := identListVar.String(); str != expectedVar {
		t.Fatalf("expected string of %#v to be %s, got %s",
			identListVar, expectedVar, str)
	}

	identList := &parser.IdentList{
		List: []*parser.Ident{
			{Name: "a"},
			{Name: "b"},
			{Name: "c"},
		},
		VarArgs: false,
	}

	expected := "(a, b, c)"
	if str := identList.String(); str != expected {
		t.Fatalf("expected string of %#v to be %s, got %s",
			identList, expected, str)
	}
}

END STEP

STEP 197 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='parser/parser.go'
s=open(p).read()
old='''func (p *Parser) parseIdentList() *IdentList {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "IdentList"))
	}

	var params []Expr
	lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
	isVarArgs := false
	if p.token != token.RParen {
		if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
			isVarArgs = true
			p.next()
		}

		if isVarArgs {
			params = append(params, p.parseIdent())
		} else {
			params = append(params, p.parsePatternParam())
		}
		for !isVarArgs && p.token == token.Comma {
			p.next()
			if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
				isVarArgs = true
				p.next()
			}
			if isVarArgs {
				params = append(params, p.parseIdent())
			} else {
				params = append(params, p.parsePatternParam())
			}
		}
	}

	rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
	return &IdentList{
		LParen:  lparen,
		RParen:  rparen,
		VarArgs: isVarArgs,
		List:    params,
	}
}
'''
new='''func (p *Parser) parseIdentList() *IdentList {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "IdentList"))
	}

	var (
		params   []*Ident
		patterns []Expr
	)
	lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
	isVarArgs := false
	addParam := func(param Expr) {
		if ident, ok := param.(*Ident); ok {
			params = append(params, ident)
			patterns = append(patterns, nil)
		} else {
			params = append(params, &Ident{
				Name:    fmt.Sprintf("$param%d", len(params)),
				NamePos: param.Pos(),
			})
			patterns = append(patterns, param)
		}
	}
	if p.token != token.RParen {
		if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
			isVarArgs = true
			p.next()
		}

		if isVarArgs {
			addParam(p.parseIdent())
		} else {
			addParam(p.parsePatternParam())
		}
		for !isVarArgs && p.token == token.Comma {
			p.next()
			if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
				isVarArgs = true
				p.next()
			}
			if isVarArgs {
				addParam(p.parseIdent())
			} else {
				addParam(p.parsePatternParam())
			}
		}
	}

	rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
	return &IdentList{
		LParen:   lparen,
		RParen:   rparen,
		VarArgs:  isVarArgs,
		List:     params,
		Patterns: patterns,
	}
}
'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)

+
+// isPatternLHS reports whether the bracketed expression currently being parsed
+// is followed by an assignment operator (:= or =) and should therefore be
+// parsed as a destructuring pattern.
+func (p *Parser) isPatternLHS() bool {
+	offset := p.scanner.offset
+	s := NewScanner(p.file, p.scanner.src, nil, 0)
+	s.offset = offset
+	s.readOffset = offset
+	s.lineOffset = offset
+	s.ch = ' '
+	s.next()
+
+	depth := 1
+	for {
+		tok, _, _ := s.Scan()
+		switch tok {
+		case token.LBrack, token.LBrace, token.LParen:
+			depth++
+		case token.RBrack, token.RBrace, token.RParen:
+			depth--
+			if depth == 0 {
+				tok2, _, _ := s.Scan()
+				return tok2 == token.Define || tok2 == token.Assign
+			}
+		case token.EOF:
+			return false
+		case token.Semicolon:
+			if depth == 0 {
+				return false
+			}
+		}
 	}
 }

 		defer untracep(tracep(p, "SimpleStmt"))
 	}
 
-	x := p.parseExprList()
+	x := p.parseLHSExprList()

 	case token.Assign, token.Define: // assignment statement
@@ -1021,6 +1223,23 @@ func (p *Parser) parseSimpleStmt(forIn bool) Stmt {

+func (p *Parser) parseLHSExprList() (list []Expr) {
+	if p.trace {
+		defer untracep(tracep(p, "LHSExpressionList"))
+	}
+
+	if (p.token == token.LBrack || p.token == token.LBrace) &&
+		p.isPatternLHS() {
+		list = append(list, p.parsePattern())
+		for p.token == token.Comma {
+			p.next()
+			list = append(list, p.parsePattern())
+		}
+		return
+	}
+	return p.parseExprList()
+}
